台灣股市：訂單持續暢旺突破千億，AI 需求持續向供應鏈擴散，廠商展望樂觀
9 月觀察台股基本面，最明確的支撐來自 8 月外銷訂單創下單月新高，以及企業對 9 月接單維持
擴張預期。經濟部公布，8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6 億美元，月增 5.1%、年增 71.4%；其中資訊通信
與電子產品合計 799.6 億美元，占整體 77.7%，顯示 AI 伺服器、半導體與雲端建設仍是主要成長來
源。受查廠商對 9 月接單的預期，以訂單金額計算的動向指數為 52.1，高於代表持平的 50，意味整
體接單有望較 8 月增加。雖然 9 月實際結果仍待公布，但已發布的數據與企業展望，為台股進入第

四季提供了正面的基本面依據。
更值得留意的是，AI 需求正從核心晶片延伸至更廣的供應鏈。雲端業者擴充運算能力，除了帶動
伺服器與半導體接單，也使自研 ASIC、IC 設計及相關電子零組件成為後續觀察重點；半導體投資與
設備擴產，則為自動化設備、銅箔及高階材料創造機會。8 月機械與基本金屬外銷訂單分別年增 28.3%
及 26.8%，顯示科技投資的效益已反映在部分非電子貨品。對台股而言，這有助於投資主線從少數大
型權值股，延伸至具技術優勢與訂單能見度的中上游企業。不過，面板需求仍偏弱，產業表現並非全
面同步，選股仍須辨別企業實際受惠程度。
展望 10 月，市場焦點可望轉向第三季財報、第四季營運展望，以及 9 月外銷訂單能否驗證接單
續強。就廠商對 9 月的預期而言，資訊通信與電子產品的訂單金額動向指數分別為 52.4 及 51.5，顯
示科技需求仍具支撐；基本金屬與化學品則達 59.0 及 61.0，呈現部分材料領域接單改善的期待。若
AI 伺服器出貨、自研晶片開發及半導體資本支出持續推進，相關台灣供應鏈的營收與獲利有望受惠。

另一方面，機械與光學器材指數分別為 46.3 及 49.5，提醒市場不能將整體訂單成長視為所有產業同
步復甦，仍須以後續實績檢驗。
投資策略方面，10 月以「掌握 AI 主線、尋找擴散機會、重視獲利驗證」作為配置方向。核心部
位可留意伺服器、半導體及 IC 設計等需求較明確的企業，並觀察 ASIC 相關業務能否由開發逐步轉
化為穩定出貨；其他機會則可從設備、銅箔與高階材料中，篩選確實取得訂單的公司。選股不只看產
業接單增幅，更應比較個別企業的營收成長、毛利率與獲利展望，避免在評價已高時追逐短期題材。
整體而言，台股仍有基本面支撐，後續行情能否擴大，關鍵在於 AI 需求能否持續轉化為更多企業的
實際獲利。

中國股市：AI 產業規模擴張難掩獲利困境，產業進入變現考驗
9 月中國 AI 產業的討論，從模型能力與使用量，逐漸轉向更根本的問題：在高階晶片取得受限、
業者競爭激烈的環境下，龐大的投入能否形成可持續的回報。9 月中美高層會談後，雙方宣布建立
AI 相關對話機制，但公開成果未顯示高階 AI 晶片出口限制已放寬。政策溝通有所進展，並未立即改
變企業取得先進算力的條件。與此同時，低價及開放權重策略持續降低 AI 使用門檻，帶動市場擴張，
也使企業更難單靠模型服務取得理想利潤。對投資人而言，9 月最重要的訊號不是單一產品的表現，

而是中國 AI 已進入需要同時回答算力效率、商業變現與資本回收的階段。
從整體結構來看，中國具備電力、製造基礎及廣泛應用場景，有利於 AI 以較低成本進入企業營
運與日常服務；但部署成本較低，不代表產業獲利便能同步提高。當業者以低價爭取用戶，使用量可
以迅速累積，收入卻可能跟不上基礎設施、研發及營運支出。以特定平台的觀察為例，DeepSeek 曾
取得可觀的代幣使用量，但相應支出占比明顯較低；這不能代表中國 AI 產業的整體財務表現，卻凸
顯「有人使用」與「願意付費」之間仍有距離。未來產業能否改善獲利，取決於企業是否找到具持續
需求的應用、建立合理收費方式，並避免同質化服務陷入反覆降價。
展望 10 月，中國 AI 的成長空間仍在，但市場衡量進展的標準應由使用規模，逐步轉向投入產出。
若 AI 更深入企業工作流程，相關需求有望延續；然而，需求增加後，業者仍須證明服務品質、運算
效率與客戶付費能同步改善。晶片限制使算力使用效率更加重要，價格競爭則使收入品質成為另一項
考驗。Vercel 資料顯示，8 月開放權重模型在其平台占代幣使用量 56%，支出占比卻為 14%，說明

高使用量未必對應高支出；10 月宜觀察企業是否開始披露更清楚的付費需求與成本改善，而非只比
較流量或模型排名。
投資策略方面，對中國 AI 宜維持關注成長、審慎評估獲利的態度，避免把產業滲透率提高，直
接等同於所有企業的投資價值提升。評估重點可放在三個整體問題：第一，持續增加的算力與研發投
入，能否換來足夠的收入；第二，低價競爭下，企業能否留住付費客戶並維持合理利潤；第三，AI
應用能否從試用走向長期採購。具備穩定需求、成本控制能力及清楚收費模式的企業，較有機會在產
業整合中建立優勢。整體而言，中國 AI 並非缺少使用場景，而是仍須證明大規模使用可以轉化為可
持續的獲利，這將是 10 月觀察產業與相關市場表現的核心。

科技類股：AI 應用加速落地，光通訊與 Agent 入口迎來成長機會
AI 產業的成長主軸正由模型訓練延伸至推論與實際應用，讓軟體服務與硬體建設形成相互帶動的
循環。當 AI 不再只回答問題，而能協助搜尋資訊、安排活動及完成跨平台任務，使用頻率與運算需
求有望提高。Meta 於 9 月推出個人 AI 助理 Muse，透過應用程式及 WhatsApp 接觸既有用戶，積
極爭取 AI 服務入口。對產業而言，Agent 普及不僅意味新的服務形式，也可能擴大雲端運算與資料
傳輸需求，使應用端的創新成為基礎設施升級的推力。投資機會可從使用者入口延伸至相關供應鏈。

在硬體端，AI 推論與多步驟任務若持續增加，資料中心內部的資料交換將更頻繁，高速且節能的
連接能力因而更具價值。光通訊除支援資料中心之間的傳輸，也逐步朝機櫃及晶片附近的互連場景發
展，拓展供應商的產品空間。Lumentum 指出，AI 運算對速度與頻寬的要求提升，帶動光連結需求，
並已在營收展望中反映相關成長。高速光模組與新型互連方案也持續推進。雖然部分新技術仍須經過
客戶導入與量產驗證，但既有產品需求與後續技術升級有望接續，讓光通訊成為 AI 基礎設施中值得
持續關注的環節。
在應用端，Muse 代表 AI 從被動回覆走向主動協助執行任務的嘗試。若 Agent 能替使用者完成
搜尋、比較、預訂與付款等流程，便利性提升有望促進使用黏著度，並為支付、交易與服務串接平台
創造新的合作機會。跨平台操作可能由單一入口整合，促使各產業重新思考客戶服務。「Muse 對各
產業的影響」圖可呈現商業模式轉變的可能方向。隨著更多服務接入 Agent 生態系，使用情境有望
持續擴展；後續若任務完成率、使用留存及付費轉換逐步改善，應用端便有機會形成更明確的收入來

源，並進一步支持運算與傳輸投資。
投資策略方面，建議以「應用入口與基礎設施並重」掌握 AI 從訓練走向推論的成長機會。硬體
方面，可優先關注已切入主要客戶供應鏈、具備高速光模組與新型互連產品布局，且訂單能見度較佳
的業者。應用方面，則可追蹤具備用戶基礎、服務整合能力與清楚收費模式的平台，觀察 Agent 能
否從新產品熱度轉化為持續使用與付費需求。配置上可先以業績較明確的基礎設施業者為核心，再逐
步增加應用平台的布局，兼顧當前需求與後續成長空間。整體而言，AI 應用愈深入日常生活與企業
流程，入口平台與光通訊供應鏈愈有機會相互受惠；選股關鍵仍是找出能將需求成長轉化為實際業績
的企業。

拉美市場：10 月巴西總統大選將登場，政治不確定性影響市場表現
9 月拉美市場維持震盪整理。一方面，拉美各國通膨數據普遍低於市場預期，有利市場降息預期；
另一方面，巴西總統大選選情持續拉鋸，政治不確定性升溫則讓投資人保持觀望。展望 10 月，隨巴
西大選投票日逼近，政治因素仍可能使拉美市場短線波動加劇。
受到美伊衝突帶動油價上漲影響，市場原本擔心燃料與運輸成本增加，會讓拉美物價再度大幅上
漲，進而迫使拉美央行升息。然而，高盛分析指出，拉美市場目前的通膨率仍受到控制，當地經濟承
受能源漲價的能力，比市場原先擔心的情況更好。其中一個重要原因是，新興市場政府透過燃油補貼、

稅制安排與零售價格管制，讓消費者實際感受到的價格漲幅相對有限。此外，上半年全球農產品供給
較為充裕，使食品價格漲幅偏低，也抵銷了部分能源漲價帶來的影響。由於拉美市場實際通膨壓力小
於原先市場預期，央行進一步大幅升息的必要性也隨之降低，高盛因此認為，市場目前對多數拉美經
濟體未來一至兩年的升息預期過於悲觀，並預期拉美各國央行在 2027 年較可能重新轉向降息。
不過，拉美多國經濟出現放緩跡象，亦可能壓抑股市上漲空間。智利央行於 9 月會議維持政策利
率 4.50%不變，並將 2026 年經濟成長預測下修至 0.25%至 0.75%，低於 6 月預估的 1.0%至 1.75%。
智利央行指出，第二季內需成長放緩，勞動市場及信心指標同步轉弱，加上燃料價格上漲侵蝕企業與
家庭所得，均對經濟活動形成壓力。巴西經濟亦出現放緩跡象，巴西央行於 9 月 16 日一致決議調降
Selic 政策利率 25 個基點至 13.75%，並指出景氣循環產業動能轉弱，且整體與核心通膨均已回落至
目標區間上限以下，為進一步降息提供空間。不過，巴西央行並未承諾下次會議是否將繼續降息，而

是強調將視未來經濟與通膨數據逐次評估。
另一方面，巴西總統大選進入白熱化階段則是當前影響市場最主要的因子。市場民調顯示，相較
7 月時現任總統魯拉在民調中普遍領先競爭者波索納洛約 5 至 8 個百分點，在 9 月的民調雙方差距已
縮小至 2 個百分點以內，選情不確定性仍高。若選舉最終由魯拉勝選，其支持財政擴張的政策，將可
能使市場對於巴西財政赤字問題的擔憂加劇，進而使巴西股市面臨較大壓力；而若波索納洛勝選，市
場普遍預期其能提出改善投資人信心的財政政策，為巴西央行創造更多降息空間，有利後續巴西股市
上行。
整體而言，儘管市場對拉美各國當前升息預期可能過於悲觀，但目前拉美多國經濟成長同步放
緩，加上巴西總統大選選情膠著，選後財政政策方向不確定性高，在考量大選及政策方向不確定性的
狀況下，短線拉美市場波動恐將加劇。

能源類股：全球石油庫存持續下降，但中東石油出口已出現回升
9 月西德州原油期貨價格受美伊衝突升溫影響，一度於月中重新站上每桶 100 美元，其後隨衝突
趨緩，油價漲幅逐步收斂，月底約回落至 90 美元附近，能源類股表現亦大致跟隨油價走勢。展望 10
月，近期中東石油供應恢復速度不如預期，加上庫存持續下降，預期油價下檔仍具支撐。然而，市場
數據顯示，高油價及供應受阻已同步抑制石油需求，且中東各國原油出口持續回升，後續供應緊張情
況有望逐步改善。因此，雖然中東地緣政治風險仍將對油價形成支撐，但隨美伊重啟外交接觸，油價
進一步大幅上漲的空間預期相對有限。

受到美伊談判僵持影響，國際能源署（IEA）與美國能源資訊署（EIA）在 9 月月報均將中東石油
供應恢復的預期時間延後。IEA 將中東石油供應全面恢復的預期時間延至 2027 年，並將 2026 年全
球石油供應減幅，由上月預估的每日減少 430 萬桶擴大至 570 萬桶；EIA 則將中東石油供給恢復至
衝突前水準的時間，由 2027 年初延至第 2 季。
此外，受到中東供應受阻與油價偏高影響，IEA 亦將 2026 年全球石油需求預測下修每日 94 萬桶，
預估全年需求將較去年減少每日 250 萬桶，EIA 則預估全年石油需求將減少約每日 170 萬桶，石油
輸出國組織（OPEC）也將今年需求由上月預估的每日增加 60 萬桶下修至 40 萬桶。
受此影響，全球石油庫存持續下降。IEA 估計，8 月全球可觀察石油庫存減少 9,500 萬桶，自 2
月底以來已累計下降 5.07 億桶，顯示市場仍持續動用庫存填補缺口。EIA 也預期全球石油庫存將持
續下降至年底，因此將 2026 年下半年布蘭特原油均價預測，由上月約每桶 82 美元上調至 90 美元；

此外，隨著 2027 年中東出口與產量逐步恢復，IEA 預估明年全年布蘭特原油均價將回落至 74 美元。
另一方面，中東原油出口則已經出現回升跡象。路透社 9 月 28 日引述 Kpler 初步資料指出，沙
烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋 9 月原油出口均出現上升，帶動中東主要產油國當月原油日均出口量回升至每日
1,280 萬桶，創下 2 月戰爭爆發以來最高水準。其中，沙烏地阿拉伯 9 月原油日均出口預估將達每日
540 萬桶，較 8 月的 244.6 萬桶增加逾一倍。由於沙國東西向輸油管線先前因攻擊受損，沙烏地計
畫將部分原先經紅海延布港出口的原油，轉往波斯灣沿岸港口裝運，帶動拉斯坦努拉港 9 月日均出貨
量預估由 8 月的每日 92.9 萬桶增至 360 萬桶。
整體而言，中東供應恢復時程延後，加上全球石油庫存持續下降，短期仍有利支撐油價。然而，
石油需求預估同步下修，且沙烏地阿拉伯與阿聯酋原油出口均已逐步回升，後續石油供應緊張程度可
望有所緩解。整體而言，儘管油價仍受中東地緣風險支撐，但隨美國期中選舉接近，近期美伊亦重啟

外交接觸，市場對原油供應中斷的擔憂可望逐步降溫，油價進一步大幅上漲空間相對有限。

黃金類股：升息預期攀升壓抑金價表現；財富配置及央行買盤支撐中長期需求
近期黃金價格表現較為疲弱，主要受到市場升息預期持續攀升，帶動美債殖利率走高，增加持有
黃金的機會成本，對於利率較為敏感的黃金 ETF 需求因此受到壓抑。然而，高盛研究指出，聯準會
近期決議升息雖然延長了市場高利率環境時間，但並不會進一步墊高利率終點，加上全球央行持續購
金，以及投資人從財富配置角度增加黃金配置，均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。
高盛報告指出，聯準會於 9 月升息一碼，增加持有黃金的機會成本，使對利率敏感的黃金 ETF 需
求下滑，高盛因此將 2026 年底金價預估由每盎司 4,900 美元下修至 4,650 美元。然而，高盛也預估，

聯準會將於 2027 年 9 月至 2028 年 3 月期間降息三碼，使政策利率最終回到 3.25%至 3.5%區間，
故儘管聯準會 9 月升息延長了高利率環境，但實際並未墊高利率終點，因此來自黃金 ETF 的賣壓僅
會延後金價上漲時程，但並未改變高盛對黃金的偏多看法。
另一方面，央行買盤仍是金價支撐核心。高盛 9 月報告預估，全球央行近三個月經季節調整後的
購金速度已上升至每月 91 噸，遠高於 2022 年前每月 17 噸的平均水準。高盛指出，這股需求主要
來自分散外匯儲備的考量，在 2022 年俄羅斯央行資產遭美國凍結後，各國央行更加重視儲備資產遭
制裁或凍結的風險，相較於對利率敏感的 ETF 資金，央行基於儲備分散而進行的購金，不易因短期
利率變動而轉向，因此即使聯準會升息，央行買盤仍有利支撐金價表現。
除了央行買盤外，花旗也從全球財富配置的角度看好黃金。花旗指出，全球每年新增黃金礦產供
應規模相當有限，若全球家庭額外將 0.1%的財富配置於黃金，就相當於約 4,459 噸黃金購買量，也

等於 1.2 年的新增礦產供應量。換言之，即使全球投資人僅小幅提高黃金配置比重，也可能創造遠超
過新增供給的需求。由於黃金供給短期內難以快速增加，市場往往需要透過金價上漲，吸引既有持有
人釋出黃金以滿足新增需求，因此資產配置需求增加可能對金價產生顯著推升影響。
不過，花旗也指出，黃金新增供給有限的特性雖有助於放大需求對金價的推升效果，但同時也可
能提高金價出現較劇烈修正的風險。花旗估計，2022 年以來的金價上漲已使既有黃金持有人的帳面
財富增加約 13 兆美元，若持有人將其中約 1 兆美元轉回股票、債券或現金，所形成的黃金賣壓便可
能超過目前全部實體黃金需求，故當持有人集中獲利了結時，金價也可能出現較劇烈的修正。
整體而言，在市場升息預期持續攀升的情況下，短期黃金 ETF 需求可能受到壓抑，金價仍可能面
臨震盪。然而，全球央行基於外匯儲備分散需求持續購金，加上投資人從財富配置角度提高黃金配置，
均有助於支撐中期黃金需求。

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同意，
不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
固定收益：聯準會升息穩定通膨預期，凸顯中天期美債與投資級債吸引力
美國 9 月聯準會貨幣政策會議以 12 比 0 全票通過，將聯邦基金利率目標區間上調 25 個基點至
3.75%–4.00%。最新經濟預測同步將 2026 年核心 PCE 上修至 3.4%、GDP 成長率上修至 2.3%，且
全面上修 2026 至 2027 年政策利率預估至 4.1%，並暗示年底前仍有一次升息空間。9 月聯準會採取
預防性升息，主要是在避免通膨預期鬆動並維持政策公信力，確立高利率維持更久的政策基調。
美國政府於 9 月 30 日公布第二季國內生產毛額(GDP)終值，經季節調整後成長年率由 1.5%上修

至 2.2%，高於經濟學家預估的維持不變。第一季 GDP 季增年率也由 2.1%上修至 2.5%。其中，占
美國經濟活動逾 6 成的消費支出，第二季成長率由 3.4%上修至 3.8%，顯示美國消費動能強勁；元
大投顧指出，美國 8 月核心資本財訂單月增率加速至 1.6%，其中初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均
呈現增長，顯示 AI 相關投資及企業設備更新需求持續支撐資本財訂單，企業設備投資可望對經濟成
長形成穩健貢獻。在消費與企業動能表現強勁下，使市場重新評估美國經濟對通膨黏性的影響。
彭博 10 月 1 日的報告指出，在 Market Pulse 調查的 173 名受訪者，超過一半的人預測美國 30
年期公債殖利率在年底將達到 6%。儘管伊朗戰爭和高借貸成本持續影響，美國經濟仍維持強勁成長，
這種韌性加劇了通膨和進一步升息的⾵險；人工智慧基礎設施建設競賽引發了企業發債的狂潮，推升
長天期債券發行量，全球企業今年以來已發行超過 4000 億美元的債券，用於技術投資，而其中大部
分債券發行於美國。另外，各國政府持續的預算赤字，美國聯邦政府債務規模首次突破 40 兆美元，

美國政府需要擴大發債量，投資人要求更高的利率來購買債券等因素，皆為推升美國長天期殖利率上
行的原因。因此，長天期債券後續表現仍須持續觀察利率走勢、債券供需變化及通膨發展。
展望 10 月，由於美國通膨仍高於聯準會 2%的目標，2026 年底有再升息一碼的可能，使得短
天期利率下行空間有限。在政策利率可能維持高檔、長天期殖利率又受到供給面壓力推升的環境下，
當前的債券配置應以「息收防禦」與「存續期間控管」為核心。隨著美國公債利率上行，彭博美國投
資級債指數平均剩餘到期期限持續下降，存續期間縮短有助降低利率變動對債券價格的影響。美國債
市投資建議重點配置中天期美國公債與投資級公司債。中天期美國公債與投資級公司債不僅能鎖定具
吸引力的收益率水準，也能兼顧存續期間風險控管，降低利率波動對投資組合淨值的衝擊，在當前環
境下有助於兼顧收益與風險控制。

## Document 066: 2026年10月社融数据点评：政策托底下信用扩张磨底，内生融资需求仍显不足

ooo[Table_ReportInfo] 2026 年 11 月 13 日      
证 券研究报告•宏观简评报告     
数据点评 
政策托底下信用扩张磨底，内生融资需求仍显不足 
——2026 年 10 月社融数据点评

点评

西 南证券研究院 
[Table_Summary]  摘要：总体来看，10 月社会融资规模存量同比增长 7.2%，增速较上月回升 0.1
个百分点，为 2025 年 8 月以来首次回升，单月新增社融 1.14 万亿元，同比多
增 3251 亿元，低基数叠加政府债券融资大幅多增是主要支撑。10 月政府债券
融资新增 1.18 万亿元，同比多增 6983 亿元，其中国债净融资明显提速，中央
加杠杆成为政府债融资的主要支撑；地方专项债发行节奏边际放缓，年内剩余
额度仍有待释放。企业债券净融资同比多增 946 亿元，低利率环境下直接融资
保持韧性；委托贷款同比多增 208 亿元，主要受新型政策性金融工具以股东借
款方式注入项目资本金带动。间接融资方面，10 月人民币贷款减少 5846 亿元，
同比多减 5692 亿元，主要受票据融资集中到期拖累。企业短期贷款和中长期
贷款分别同比多增 2000 亿元、1300 亿元，但整体投放规模仍偏弱，政策性金
融工具对企业融资的带动作用逐步显现；居民贷款减少 4300 亿元，同比多减
696 亿元，居民中长期贷款多减，个人住房贷款余额连续 14 个季度负增长，
房贷长期净偿还趋势延续。展望后续，政府债融资及结存限额发行仍将支撑社
融，企业中长期贷款有望受益于政策性金融工具逐步落地，但居民信贷修复仍
受地产市场调整制约，社融改善仍较依赖财政及政策发力。存款端，10 月人
民币存款同比多增 6000 亿元，居民存款同比少减、非银存款同比少增，反映
居民存款向非银资管渠道再配置的速度边际放缓。10 月末 M2、M1 同比分别
增长 7.1%、4.4%，M2 增速小幅回落，M1 增速明显回升，M2-M1 剪刀差收
窄至 2.7 个百分点，货币活化程度有所改善。但 M1 回升仍受到低基数及财政
支出的阶段性支撑，实体需求尚未实质性改善，后续货币活化程度能否持续提
升仍取决于信贷内生动能的修复。 
 社融存量增速迎一年多来首次回升，低基数与政府债托底社融增量。从社融存
量看，2026 年 10 月，社会融资规模存量同比上升 7.2%，增速相比上月加快
0.1 个百分点，为 2025 年 8 月以来增速首次边际回升。从社融增量看，10 月
单月的社会融资规模增量为 1.14 万亿元，同比多增 3251 亿元，主要受低基数
（2025 年 10 月社融增量仅为 8178 亿元）叠加政府债融资支撑影响。10 月单
月的人民币贷款减少 5846 亿元，同比多减 5692 亿元。10 月社融同比多增主
要依靠政府债券融资拉动，其中国债融资明显提速，地方专项债发行有所放缓，
社融支撑更多来自中央加杠杆。信贷总量同比转负，主要受票据融资收缩拖累；
企业短贷和中长贷虽同比多增，但整体规模偏弱，反映信贷呈现政策托底下的
弱修复。居民中长贷同比多减，显示居民房贷偿还压力仍大，主动降杠杆的取
向尚未改变。10 月下旬以来，央行密集推出政策工具并明确货币政策基调，
先后设立债市非银大额赎回互换便利，将主要政策利率锚由 7 天期逆回购切换
至隔夜逆回购；11 月初联合金融监管总局、证监会召开专题座谈会，优化互换
便利、股票回购增持再贷款两项资本市场支持工具的操作模式与利率定价机
制。11 月发布的三季度货币政策执行报告重申实施适度宽松货币政策，结构性
工具新增“六张网”建设投向，深化资本市场政策工具箱协同发力。预计四季
度社融仍将呈现财政托底、内生需求偏弱的格局。政府债券及结存限额的集中
发行将成为社融增长的主要支撑。信贷方面预计仅呈现弱修复态势：地产市场
调整制约居民中长期贷款改善，企业中长期贷款则更多依靠 PSL 及政策性工

### Page 2

请务必阅读正文后的重要声明部分
2
具定向支持。整体来看，四季度宽信用仍主要依靠财政发力，剔除政府债券融
资后，实体经济融资需求依然偏弱。
 国债放量支撑政府债融资，政策性工具托底委托贷款。从社融结构看，2026
年 10 月，直接融资新增 1.56 万亿元，同比多增 7581 亿元，主要受政府债券
融资支撑。从分项看，政府债融资方面，10 月政府债融资新增 1.18 万亿元，
同比多增 6983 亿元。10 月单月国债净融资 7727 亿元，同比去年同期的 2245
亿元提升超三倍，中央国债加速放量。10 月地方政府新增专项债发行 3280 亿
元，同比涨幅收窄至 14.13%，发行进度 88.07%，慢于去年同期 2.03 个百分
点，地方专项债发行节奏边际放缓。企业直接融资方面，10 月单月企业债券
净融资增加 3446 亿元，同比多增 946 亿元，主要受利率下行环境下企业融资
成本降低影响。非金融企业境内股票融资增加 347 亿元，同比少增 348 亿元，
股权融资小幅收缩。表外融资方面，10 月表外融资减少 240 亿元，同比少减

846 亿元。其中，委托贷款增加 1862 亿元，同比多增 208 亿元，核心驱动是
新型政策性金融工具以“股东借款”模式注入项目资本金。信托贷款减少 415
亿元，同比多减 570 亿元。未贴现银行承兑汇票减少 1687 亿元，同比少减 1208
亿元。承兑规模收缩叠加表内信贷分流持续压制票据融资需求，去年同期基数
偏低导致本期同比少减。往后看，政府债方面，截至 11 月 12 日，今年地方政
府专项债已发行 3.88 万亿元，年内剩余发行额度为 5249 亿元。后续需关注国
债放量后，地方政府专项债能否在年底至明年初接续放量。如果专项债进度持
续偏慢，政府债对社融的托底力度存在边际衰减的风险。低利率环境为企业债
提供发行支撑，但实体盈利预期尚未实质性修复，自发融资扩张动能偏弱。
 票据集中到期拉低净融资额，非银资金需求阶段性上行。企业端，2026 年 10
月，企（事）业单位贷款减少 1600 亿元，同比多减 5100 亿元。企业贷款自
2026 年 7 月单月减少 1300 亿元后，本月再度负增且减少幅度有所扩大，主要

由票据融资大幅多减导致。其中，企业短期贷款增加 100 亿元，同比多增 2000
亿元。企业中长期贷款增加 1600 亿元，同比多增 1300 亿元。企业短贷、中
长贷同比均实现多增，指向实体融资需求边际回暖，但投放规模中枢仍处低位，
整体呈现政策托底下的弱修复特征。票据融资减少 3117 亿元，同比多减 8123
亿元。票据的“滚续期”通常在 6 个月左右。今年二季度票据融资总额达到
1.91 万亿元，同比去年二季度多增了 1.42 万亿元，4 月单月票据融资额更是
达到 1.24 万亿元，处于近年内高位水平。这些票据在四季度（尤其是 10 月）
集中到期，形成了巨大的兑付压力，叠加 9 月末银行冲量行为退潮，共同导致
本月票据融资大幅减少。非银贷款增加 3470 亿元，同比多增 2534 亿元。11 月
初央行牵头召开资本市场两项支持工具座谈会、加码实施股票回购增持再贷
款，非银资金需求阶段性上行，或源于资本市场维稳相关诉求。居民端，10
月居民贷款减少 4300 亿元，同比多减 696 亿元，居民端信贷需求整体偏弱的

格局没有改变。其中，居民短期贷款减少 2700 亿元，同比少减 166 亿元。居
民中长期贷款减少 1563 亿元，同比多减 863 亿元。截至 2026 年三季度末，
个人住房贷款余额 35.91 万亿元，同比下降 4.1%，连续 14 个季度负增长，房
贷长期净偿还趋势深化。后续看，政策性金融工具资金拨付和形成配套融资需
求之间存在时滞，银行开始筹备明年“开门红”，预计四季度企业中长贷将处
于季节性偏弱阶段。10 月我国 30 大中城市成交面积为 600.84 万平方米，同
比下降 18.14%，降幅较上月扩大 7.39 个百分点。新房销售尚未迎来趋势性回
暖，居民信贷同比多减的压力有望在政策托底下得到阶段性缓释，然而信贷由
负转正仍有较大距离。
 “存款搬家”有所降速，货币活化程度边际改善。从存款端来看，2026 年 10
月，人民币存款增加 1.21 万亿元，同比多增 6000 亿元。分存款类型看，居民
存款减少 9200 亿元，同比少减 4200 亿元。在居民收入预期未见实质性回暖

的背景下，存款规模回落或反映居民优先压降存量债务、保障现金流稳定，同
时伴随储蓄资金向理财等非银资管渠道再配置的特征。非金融企业存款减少
1.25 万亿元，是四类存款中收缩幅度最大的一项，同比多减 1647 亿元，主要

### Page 3

请务必阅读正文后的重要声明部分
3
源于信贷派生偏弱叠加季度缴税资金出库的共同拖累。财政存款增加 1.4 万亿
元，同比多增 6800 亿元，主要受季度主体税种集中入库带动。非银行业金融
机构存款增加 1.58 万亿元，同比少增 2700 亿元。非银存款已连续多月同比少
增，显示居民存款搬家的速度在放缓。从货币端来看，10 月末，M2 同比增速
收窄 0.1 个百分点至 7.1%，低于市场预期的 7.2%，主要系信贷派生走弱，同
时缴税、购债带来实体资金回流国库，而国库沉淀资金不计入 M2。M1 同比
增速扩大 1.2 个百分点至 4.4%，高于市场预期的 3.5%，主要靠低基数与财政
支出沉淀的临时支撑，而非实体需求自发回暖。10 月末，M2-M1 同比剪刀差
收窄 1.3 个百分点至 2.7 个百分点，显示企业活期资金压力边际缓解、资金活
化程度有所提升。在信贷内生动能未实质修复之前，M1 反弹的斜率更可能呈
现“台阶式”而非“趋势性”，剪刀差进一步收窄的空间不宜高估。
 风险提示：政策落地节奏不及预期风险，海外经济超预期波动风险。已公布的

宏观或产业政策在实际执行过程中，可能因配套措施不完善、部门协调不畅、
地方执行力度差异或市场环境变化等因素，导致其推进速度、覆盖范围或最终
效果慢于或弱于市场预期，从而影响政策目标的实现与经济修复的进程；主要
经济体的政治局势、经济数据或宏观政策出现意外变化，其幅度或频率超出市
场普遍预测。这种超预期波动可能引发全球金融市场动荡、贸易条件恶化或外
需急剧收缩，并通过金融、贸易及预期渠道向国内传导，增加我国外部环境的
复杂性与不稳定性。

分析师承诺 
本报告署名分析师具有中国证券业协会授予的证券投资咨询执业资格并注册为证券分析师，报告所采用的数据均
来自合法合规渠道，分析逻辑基于分析师的职业理解，通过合理判断得出结论，独立、客观地出具本报告。分析师承
诺不曾因，不因，也将不会因本报告中的具体推荐意见或观点而直接或间接获取任何形式的补偿。

投资评级说明 
报告中投资建议所涉及的评级分为公司评级和行业评级（另有说明的除外）。评级标准为报告发布日后 6 个月内
的相对市场表现，即：以报告发布日后 6 个月内公司股价（或行业指数）相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的涨跌幅
作为基准。其中：A 股市场以沪深 300 指数为基准，新三板市场以三板成指（针对协议转让标的）或三板做市指数（针
对做市转让标的）为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准；美国市场以纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数为基准。 
公司评级 
买入：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在 20%以上 
持有：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 10%与 20%之间 
中性：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于-10%与 10%之间 
回避：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于-20%与-10%之间 
卖出：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在-20%以下 
行业评级 
强于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报高于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 5%以上 
跟随大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报介于同期相关证券市场代表性指数-5%与 5%之间 
弱于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报低于同期相关证券市场代表性指数-5%以下

## Document 119: 261110_daiwa_avc

Source PDF: all report pdf/261110_daiwa_avc.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: AVC announced its 3Q26 results and hosted an investor 
conference on 10 November 2026 after market hours. We believe AVC will 
be one of the key beneficiaries from the rising penetration rate of liquid 
cooling in both ASIC and GPU projects, with an aggressive capacity 
expansion plan over 2026-28. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our latest 
Flash, 3Q26 results preview: likely to beat expectations, on 2 November).

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results beat the Bloomberg consensus. 
AVC’s 3Q26 revenue of TWD59,089m (+20.3% QoQ; +51.8% YoY) was 
roughly in line with our and the Bloomberg consensus estimates. Its 3Q26 
gross margin came in at 33.9% (vs. 32.6% in 2Q26 and 26.1% in 3Q25), a 
record-high level, higher than our estimate (33.5%) and the consensus 
(33.3%) on better product mix and high server revenue contribution of 66% 
in 3Q26 vs. 66% in 2Q26 and 54% in 3Q25. Its operating profit of 
TWD16,856m (+25.1% QoQ; +108.5% YoY) was in line with our estimate 
and beat the consensus by 4.2%. Thus, its operating margin of 28.5% (vs. 
27.4% in 2Q26 and 20.8% in 3Q25), a record-high level, was also in line 
with our estimate and beat the consensus (27.9%). With an FX loss of 
TWD304m, its net profit of TWD11,564m (+20.9% QoQ; +116.6% YoY) was 
in line with our estimate and beat the market consensus estimate by 3%. Its 
3Q26 EPS finished at TWD29.32.

Key highlights from the investor conference – liquid cooling revenue 
from AI servers continue to rise. AVC is confident it can maintain its 
leading position as a thermal and mechanical solution integrator, being a 
primary design partner for ASIC and GPU projects. In 2027, AVC expects 
its revenue from ASICs projects to be higher than GPU ones. From 3Q26, 
AVC has started shipping the double-sided cold plates, which it expects to 
be adopted by all ASIC clients and some GPU clients. With years of 
experience in providing CDU parts, AVC has also started shipping complete 
CDU solutions from 3Q26. AVC aims for TWD15-20bn capex in 2027 
(mainly in Vietnam, for liquid cooling capacity expansion), up from 
TWD15bn in 2026E. We keep our 2026-28E EPS broadly unchanged.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and 12M TP of 
TWD4,815, still based on a target PER of 30x vs. its past-3-year PER 
range of 5-25x [average PER of 15x]) applied to our 1-year-forward EPS. 
Downside risks: slower-than-expected progress in high-gross-margin 
orders, slow AI server adoption, and strong competition.

How we differ: Our 2028E EPS is 8% above consensus, likely on our 
more aggressive gross- and operating margins.

10 November 2026

3Q26 review: liquid cooling penetration rate rises

➢ 3Q26 EPS beat the Bloomberg consensus by 3% 
➢ Started shipments of double-sided cold plates and CDUs from 3Q26 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call with an unchanged 12M TP of TWD4,815

Company profile 
AVC is a global leading comprehensive thermal solutions provider in segments related to desktops, 
laptops, communication, servers, energy and autos. Its main product line includes thermal products 
(thermal modules and fans), chassis, system assembly and peripheral products and hinges. The 
company has been listed on Taiwan’s main board since September 2002.

ESG analysis

Update Date: 6 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and Chairperson of AVC are the same individual, but we see no issue with its board
quality. Besides, we see the Chairperson has 24 years of experience in this industry, which is a
positive sign from an executive perspective.
Capital management 2 AVC's dividend payout ratio was 40-52% over 2020-25, likely due to its capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was 1-3% of total revenue during 2021-25, and we found no suspicious activity.
E Supply chain management 1
AVC has introduced Hazardous Substance Process Management (HSPM) and received
QC080000 certification. In addition, AVC set up a Green Product team to promote HSPM and
formulate environmental regulation to make sure its material sourcing and efficiency are well-
performed.
E Data security 1
To prevent the interruption of information systems and leakage of sensitive information causing
losses to the company and customers, AVC has introduced an information security management
system and passed the ISO 27001 information security management system certification in
2019.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
4Q26 iPhone builds unchanged: We maintain our 4Q26 iPhone build forecast at
72M units (+33% q/q, -5% y/y). While builds are expected to increase sequentially,
volumes remain below last year’s level, with softer legacy-model production,
including the iPhone 16/17e, weighing on the outlook. Our 2H26 build forecast for

the new iPhone 18 family remains unchanged at 71M units (-21% y/y), with the y/y
comparison also affected by one fewer SKU launched than last year. Within this, we
continue to expect 7M units of iPhone Duo builds. We will continue to monitor the
production ramp and sell-through data for potential upside or downside to our build
estimates. Our full-year forecast implies CY26 iPhone shipments of 234M units
(+3% y/y), with modest growth for the year despite the softer 4Q y/y outlook.

4Q26 iPad builds unchanged; year-end production outlook remains subdued: We
maintain our 4Q26 iPad build forecast at 10.3M units (-10% q/q, -26% y/y), implying
continued pressure on production volumes into year-end. Our full-year estimate
remains 46.8M units (-14% y/y), with the sharper y/y contraction in 4Q pointing to a
weaker finish to the year.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.

below.

李佳欣 
(02)2563-1666  
chelsea.lee@firstsec.com.tw

奇鋐(3017) 
買進, TP 4,285  
買進 
Price(11/11):3,040 
Target Price：4285

奇鋐 10 月營收 221.03 億元(MoM + 5.16%，YoY + 34.35%)，展
望 4Q26，受惠 NVIDIA Vera Rubin、AWS Trainium3 及 Google
新一代 TPU 等 GPU 與 ASIC 平台持續拉貨，液冷產品出貨放量，
加上高 ASP 產品占比提升，預期營收及毛利率可望持續季增。展
望 2027 年，隨 CSP 持續擴大自研 ASIC 投資，預期 ASIC 營收貢
獻將超越 GPU，且主要 GPU 及 ASIC 運算晶片水冷滲透率將持續
提升。配合垂直供電(VPD)架構導入，雙面水冷板需求將明顯增
加，預期 2027 年至少過半主要客戶採用，帶動冷板用量及單機散
熱產值提升。產能方面，目前水冷月產能約 100 萬件，規劃 2027
年擴增逾 50%，並以越南為主要擴產基地，同步擴充機殼及 CDU
產能。此外，CDU 已開始出貨中國客戶，歐美客戶持續送樣驗證，
並積極布局雙相液冷及新型高導熱材料。太空散熱方面，公司已與
新舊客戶展開合作，目前仍處於 NPI 及樣品測試階段，預期產品
ASP 將優於現有產品，長期有望成為新成長動能。綜合以上，研
究部預估奇鋐 2026 年營業收入為 2,217.44 億元(YoY +58.80%)，
毛利率為 32.60%(YoY +6.79ppt)，營業利益率為 27.34%(YoY 
+7.61ppt)，稅後純益為 414.40 億元(YoY +115.99%)，EPS 為
105.07 元；2027 年營業收入為 3,402.77 億元(YoY +53.45%)，毛
利率為 34.41%(YoY +1.81ppt) ，營業利益率為 29.38%(YoY 
+2.04ppt)，稅後純益為 676.24 億元(YoY +63.18%)，EPS 為
171.46 元。公司近三年歷史本益比區間 10~25 倍，考量 ASIC 需
求擴張及水冷滲透率持續提升，帶動單機散熱產值成長，研究部以
2027 年 EPS 給予 25 倍本益比，目標價 4285 元，投資建議為買
進。

⚫ 公司基本資料 奇鋐(3017.TT)公司簡介 
奇鋐科技股份有限公司(3017.TT)成立於 1991 年，2002 年掛牌上市，為
國內營收規模最大的散熱模組製造商。公司產品涵蓋風扇、散熱模組、
石墨片、熱管、均熱板、水冷板、機殼與機櫃，亦具備整合組裝、觸控
模組及相機模組能力，提供完整散熱解決方案與系統整合服務。公司業
務應用涵蓋桌上型電腦、筆記型電腦、伺服器、通訊設備(基地台、Switch)
與手機等多元終端，並自 2019 年起大舉進軍伺服器市場，陸續取得中系

4576 大銀微(HMC)

報告日期：2026/11/12 研 究 員：洪 國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
 在半導體先進封裝、AI 及矽光子等高階應用需求暢旺，其中
微米與奈米級定位系統交期 5~6 個月，精密運動及控制元件交
期約 3~4 個月，呈現供不應求。 
 受惠於晶圓大廠持續擴充先進製程、後段設備廠訂單持續成
長，大銀微在超高精度平台訂單已經看到 2027 年年中，另外
搭配新產能將於 2Q27 投產。預估 2027 年微米與奈米級定位
系統營收金額 31.5 億元，YoY+28.0%。 
一. 公告 3Q26 稅後 EPS 2.14 元 
大銀微 3Q26 營收金額 12.18 億元，YoY+76.73%，QoQ+19.01%，
營業利益 2.96 億元，YoY+312.6%，QoQ+61.12%，稅後盈餘 2.57
億元，YoY+255.8%，QoQ+123.72%，稅後 EPS 2.14 元。 
二. 預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 2.44 元 
大銀微 10/2026 營收金額 4.10 億元，YoY+68.75%，MoM-3.96%。
在訂單呈現滿載水準，預估大銀微 4Q26 營收金額 12.84 億元，
YoY+69.94% ， QoQ+5.37% ，預估營業利益 3.32 億元，
YoY+232.13% ， QoQ+12.21% ，預估稅後盈餘 2.92 億元，
YoY+223.25%，QoQ+13.78%，預估稅後 EPS 2.44 元。 
三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 6.50 元 
大銀微 2026 年在半導體、PCB 與矽光子檢測裝置需求延續，帶
動微米奈米級高精度定位系統訂單維持數個月的能見度。另外中
國大陸工具機谷底復甦，推升控制元件產品需求，帶動大銀微 2026
年營運成長。預估大銀微 2026 年營收 43.8 億元，YoY+61.37%，
預估稅後盈餘 7.79 億元，YoY+222.63%，預估稅後 EPS 6.50 元。 
四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 9.00 元 
大銀微 2027 年在推動的 One HIWIN Solution 單一零組件供應
商，往次系統、完整定位平台以及共同開發解決方案的角色移動，
搭配同集團上銀資源整合，將有助於大銀微產品附加價值提升。
宏遠預估大銀微 2027 年營收金額 53.00 億元，YoY+21.00%，預
估稅後盈餘 10.79 億元，YoY+38.52%，預估稅後 EPS 9.00 元。 
五. 投資建議買進，目標價 252 元 
觀察國內傳動元件同業 2027 年營收成長率亞德客 ROE 21%，營
收成長率 10%，PER 15X ；上銀 ROE 11% ，營收成長率 16%，
PER 22X，大銀微 ROE 21%，營收成長率 21%，PER 21.6X，以
ROE 與營收成長率相較同業來的佳，評價與同業相較仍在低檔水
準，故投資建議買進，目標價 252 元（28X 2027 年稅後 EPS）

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
大銀微系統股份有限公司創立於 1997 年 4 月，資本額 11.98 億
元，並於 2019 年 9 月正式掛牌上市。大銀微行屬於上銀集團旗
下的核心關係企業。大銀微聚焦精準定位與高性能驅動控制技
術，並以機電整合、產品製造及技術服務之整體能力作為核心競
爭優勢，深耕半導體、電子生產設備、智慧自動化及工具機等領
域。受惠半導體先進製程與封裝需求成長，大銀微以領先業界之
精密定位技術及系統整合方案擴大服務範圍，針對客戶需求提供
專業精密定位系統整合方案，透過技術互補與研發合作，協助全 
球頂尖半導體客戶縮短開發週期並提升產出效益。

二. 2026 年營運概況及 2027 年展望 
大銀行營收主要有精密運動及控制元件、微米與奈米級定位系統
與其他三部分。

預估 2027 年精密運動及控制元件營收金額 21.00 億元，
YoY+11.7%

精密運動及控制元件產品線包括：線性馬達、直驅馬達、真空馬
達、力矩馬達、伺服馬達、高速主軸馬達、位置量測系統等，並
搭配自行開發的驅動器及控制器，達奈米級高精度定位控制功
能。廣泛應用於半導體設備/自動化搬送/FPD 設備/PCB 設備
/AOI 檢測設備/SMT 設備/雷射加工器/各式機器手臂/工具機/電
動車相關/太陽能等不同產業。精密運動及控制元件 2025 年營收
金額 12.64 億元，YoY+3.1%。

精密運動及控制元件 1Q~3Q26 營收金額 13.83 億元，
YoY+45.43%。在中國大陸對驅動器需求高，目前處於供不應求狀
態，帶動包括線性馬達、直驅馬達、伺服馬達產能稼動率在高檔
水準，預估 2026 年精密運動及控制元件產品營收金額 18.80 億
元，YoY+48.7%。

預期 2027 年，在中國大陸工具機出口額與金屬切削設備產業仍呈
現正向發展，整體需求大於供給，訂單能見度己達 3~4 個月，預
估 2027 年精密運動及控制元件產品營收金額 21.00 億元，
YoY+11.7%。

### Page 1

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.  
Buy (Maintain)

## Document 269: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사...LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금_7b277d22

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사...LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금_7b277d22.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Earnings Review Company Analysis | 이차전지/화학 | 2026. 11. 4  
일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사 Capa 우려

AMPC 4,169억원 포함 영업이익 컨센서스 대폭 상회 
동사는 8일 매출액 9조 6,434억원(YoY 59%, QoQ 27%), AMPC 포함한 영업이익 
7,560억원(OPM 7.8%, YoY 26%, QoQ 567%)로 시장 Consensus(공정공시한 8일 
기준 3,264억원)을 대폭 상회하는 어닝 서프라이즈 발표. 회사는 ESS 출하 급증과 고
전압 Mid-Ni EV 파우치 확대, 견조한 EV향 2170-46시리즈 공급을 물량 중심을 성
장하였고, 폴란드 가동률 개선, 수익성이 견조한 원통형 물량 증가, 북미 ESS 생산성 
개선에 따른 고정비 부담 완화 및 고객사의 일회성 물량 보상금 영향으로 설명. 그러나 
일회성 물량 보상금의 구체적 금액, 반복 발생 여부는 제시하지 않아 불확실성 남겨. 
7,560억원의 영업이익에도 불구하고 영업외손실 9,872억원 발생으로 당기순손실 기
록. 이에는 소형 IT파우치 자산손상 반영되어, 가동률 개선 및 감가상각비 감소를 위해 
Capa를 조정하는 배터리 회사들의 대응 전략이 이번 실적에서 다시 확인

‘30년 ESS 수요 전망들 대비 국내사 Capa 배 이상 높아 
동사는 북미 ESS Capa 확대를 통한 탈중국 수요에 적극 대응하겠다는 전략. 파우치 
라인 추가 전환 및 신규 각형 라인 확보를 통해 북미 ESS Capa를 ‘26년말 50+에서 
‘27년 70+로 제시. 최근 타사에서 ‘26년 미국 ESS 수요를 80~100GWh, 혹은 
130+GWh로, ‘30년 두배 이상, 혹은 240+GWh로 수요 전망에 대해 답변한 바 있음. 
동사 전체 Capa 300GWh(‘25년 연말), SK On 179GWh, SDI 140GWh)임을 감안하
면 ‘30년 약 2배 수요를 가정하면 셀 3사 Capa의 32%에 달하는 수요, 만일 3배 수
요 가정하면 48.5% 수준. 북미 외 시장 가성비 중시 기조 및 중국 LFP 장악(‘25년 
97% M/S)으로 진입장벽 높음을 감안하면 미국 수요 중요. 이를 상쇄하기 위해 EV향 
배터리의 안정적인 공급 지속 필요하며, 이를 위해 동사 원통형은 TESLA의 비미국 시
장 판매량이, 파우치는 시장 자체 축소 지속 여부에 유의할 필요 
상대적으로 가장 견조한 출하량, 그러나 고평가 영역  
셀3사중 EV향 배터리 출하량 및 ESS 탑재에서 현재 가장 견조한 판매량을 보이고 있
으나, AMPC와 일회성 보상금이 영업손익의 상당부분을 견인한다는 점에서 수익성 취
약하다는 단점. 또한 ROE가 (-) 영역 임에도 PBR이 3.x내외로 거래되고 있어 고평가 
지적에서 자유롭기 어려워. 당분간 모회사인 LG화학의 PRS 발행에 따른 단기 오버행 
이슈도 관건. PBR 2.3x(‘25년대비 하락하는 ROE 감안, 20% 할인)적용 목표주가 
290,000원 제시, HOLD 유지. 주식 투자 매력 낮은 상황으로 판단

EV향 3분기 data  
이차전지에서 EV향 비중은 여전히 가장 높다. 타입별로 보면 3분기 글로벌 EV향 이차전
지에서 각형의 M/S가 83%로 지속 증가하고 있으며, 동사가 생산하는 원통형과 파우치의 
경우 합산 M/S가 17%로 하락하였다. 현재 Wh 기준 동사 EV향 배터리에서 3분기 파우
치가 55%, 원통형이 45%로 파우치 비중이 10% 원통형을 상회한다.

나타난다.

그림 9 셀 3 사 분기별 EV 향 탑재량 글로벌 M/S 추이: M/S 감소중

자료: SNE 리서치, LS증권 리서치센터

밸류에이션, 수익성 대비 고평가 영역  
동사는 ‘24년 ROE -4.9%, ‘25년 -5.2%에 이어 ‘26년 -4.4%, ‘27년 -3.4%로 (-) 영역이 
지속되는 것으로 예상한다. ‘24년과 ‘25년 순이익은 (+)지만, 해외 합작회사가 많고 이들 이
익을 연결함에 따라 지배주주순이익은 적자를 기록한 것을 보인다. JV 규모가 축소되고 있
어 이러한 순이익과 지배주주 순이익상의 괴리는 좁혀지겠지만, ROE가 (-)영역 지속을 예
상함에 따라 현재 거래 PBR은 고평가 상황으로 볼 수 있다. 물론 이것은 향후 수익 성장
에 대한 시장 기대가 반영된 것으로 볼 수 있지만, 이차전지 6개사 평균 Consensus 대비 
실제 수익이 ‘23년부터 지속 하향되고 있다는 점, ESS대비 여전히 판매비중이 높은 EV에서 
출하량 정체가 일어나고 있다는 점, 미국 ESS에서 여전히 높은 중국 M/S를 감안하면 이러
한 기대 수준에 대해 다시 검토할 필요가 있다. 이에 더해, 모회사 기준에서는 필요한 과정
이지만 동사 주가 측면에서는 LG화학의 PRS 발행도 단기 오버행으로 당분간 주가에 부담
을 줄 수 있다.

그림 10 셀 3 사, 양∙음극재 3 사 추정치 변화율: ‘23 년부터 추정치 지속 하향 조정

자료: DataGuide, LS증권 리서치센터

미국 ESS가 모든 것을 해결할 수 있나? 
‘25년 글로벌 ESS 시장의 97%를 중국배터리사가 공급했다. LFP 각형이 표준화된 시장인 
것이다. 미국 시장만 중국에 대한 관세 허들, 정책 허들로 한국 배터리사에 기회로 작용한
다. 그러나 ‘26년부터 시작된 FEOC와 관세추가 부담에도 불구, 상반기 미국 ESS 시장의 
76%을 (기타사의 국적을 알 수 없기에 제외하더라도) 중국 셀사가 차지했다. 만일 한국 배
터리 유관회사들이 전망하듯 ‘30년 미국 ESS 시장이 160~200GWh 이상, 240+GWh, 혹
은 240~300GWh가 된다고 해도 3사 전체 Capa의 50%가 채 되지 못한다. EV 수요가 뒷
받침되지 않는다면 미국 ESS만으로는 가동률을 80~90% 이상 올리기 어렵다는 의미다. 특
히 동사는 원통형과 파우치를 생산하고 있으나, 원통형은 북미외 지역 TESLA의 판매에, 
파우치는 올해 출하량이 감소하는 것으로 나타나고 있다. 파우치 고객의 각형 채택 비중 
증가를 감안하면 동사 EV향 배터리 출하량 증가를 확신하기는 어렵다.   
긍정적 기대와 동시에 냉정한 점검이 필요한 시점으로 판단한다.

LS Securities Research 6 
표2 LG 에너지솔루션 3Q26 실적발표 주요 Q&A 
[Q&A]           
Q1-1. 4Q26 실적 전망과 2027 년 큰 방향은? 
  A. 4Q26 ESS 출하량은 북미 수요 대응으로 QoQ 약 40% 증가 예상 
   유럽 고전압 Mid-Ni 파우치 및 원통형 물량도 연말까지 견조한 출하 지속 전망 
   3Q26 가동을 시작한 북미 JV 효과가 더해져 전사 매출은 QoQ 약 10% 성장 가능할 것으로 예상 
   가동률 개선과 사이트 안정적 램프업은 지속 추진하나 3Q26 일회성 요인 소멸의 기저효과 존재 
   4Q26 혼다 JV ESS CAPA 추가 가동 준비에 따른 초기 고정비 부담으로 수익성 일부 둔화 불가피

2027 년 사업계획은 수립 중이며 전방 수요 변수로 구체적 가이던스 제시는 어려운 단계 
    ESS: 북미 확보 수주 기반으로 유의미한 물량 성장 지속 기대 
    소형: 전략 고객향 EV 원통형과 46 시리즈 신규 애플리케이션 판매로 안정적 성장 예상 
    EV 파우치: 중저가 제품 물량과 북미 EV 향 생산품이 더해져 성장 예상 
   전 사업부 매출 성장 기대하나 핵심 제품 경쟁력·기존 CAPA 효율화·가동률 극대화·원가 개선에 집중 
   2027 년 매출 성장률·영업이익률 등 구체적 수치는 제시 X

Q1-2. 북미 PFE 요건 충족을 위한 LFP 공급망 전략과 중국 관련 주력 양극재 공급사 대응은? 
  A. 미국 생산세액공제 확보를 위해 직접재료비 내 PFE 소재 비중 요건을 충족하는 공급망 구축 필요 
   회사 설명상 PFE 소재 허용 비중은 2026 년 40%에서 2027 년 35% 이하로 강화. Non-PFE 비중과 혼동 유의 
   소재별 소싱 전략을 구체화해 준비 중이며 요건 충족은 차질 없이 진행될 것으로 판단 
   재료비 비중이 큰 양극재는 최근 계약한 국내 업체 외 복수 Non-PFE 업체 조달 옵션 확보

음극재·분리막·전해액도 기존 Non-PFE 공급망에 더해 신규 업체 적극 발굴 
   기존 원가 중심 최적화에서 정책·지정학 리스크를 고려한 공급망 안정성 강화로 전략 확대 
   협력사의 원가·기술·물량 대응력을 종합 평가하고 장기적으로 현지 협력사 발굴·현지화 추진 
   질문한 중국 관련 주력 공급사의 개별 지위·조달 비중에 대한 직접적인 상세 답변 X

Q2-1. ESS 사업의 수익성 전망 및 개선 방안은? 
  A. EV→ESS 라인 전환으로 수요에 맞는 생산체계를 구축하고 북미 고객 적기 공급에 주력 
   다수 사이트 동시 램프업에 어려움이 있었고 고정비 절감 노력에도 수익성 개선 속도는 시장 기대보다 느렸음 
   2026 년 말 50GWh 목표 셀 라인 램프업은 마무리 국면. Link CAPA 도 3Q26 에 이어 4Q26 추가 보강 예정 
   CAPA 안정화와 점진적인 고정비 부담 완화 기대 
   확보 CAPA 효율화, 소재 소싱 다변화로 원재료비·물류비 절감에 집중 
   AI 데이터센터 및 SI 프로젝트 비중 확대를 통한 고객 포트폴리오 최적화 추진 
   셀·시스템 고용량화로 원가 경쟁력을 높여 구조적 수익성 개선 추진 
   구체적 ESS 영업이익률 목표나 개선 폭은 제시 X

Q2-2. 북미 ESS 추가 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 빠른 시장 성장 및 Non-PFE 에 우호적인 정책으로 북미 현지 생산 셀 수요 급증 
   현재 계획된 CAPA 기준으로 2028 년까지 물량은 대부분 확정. 그 이후 물량 중심으로 고객 수주 협의 진행 
   기존 전력망·유틸리티 고객, 이들을 통한 AI 데이터센터 BESS 수요, 하이퍼스케일러 BTM 직접 구매 수요 포함 
   예상 수요가 공급 가능 CAPA 를 초과해 전략적으로 중요한 프로젝트 중심으로 미래 잔여 CAPA 운영 계획 
   추가 고객명·신규 계약량 등은 제시하지 않음. 예상 수요와 확정 수주를 구분

Q3-1. 46 시리즈의 구체적인 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 기존 OEM 부터 EV 스타트업까지 이미 440GWh 이상 물량 확보 
   추가로 다수 글로벌 OEM 과 협의 중이며 가까운 시일 내 의미 있는 규모의 수주 성과 기대 
   제품 경쟁력·지역별 생산 역량으로 46 시리즈 선도 입지를 강화하고 고객 포트폴리오 확대 계획 
   440GWh 는 확보 물량이며 연간 판매량이나 생산 CAPA 가 아님. 추가 계약 규모·고객명은 미공개

### Page 7

Company Report / LG에너지솔루션 / 2026. 11.4 
LS Securities Research 7

Q3-2. Tabless 2170 의 경쟁력과 수주·신규 시장 대응 현황은? 
  A. 기존 제품 대비 낮은 발열·저항으로 안전성 및 고출력에 유리해 HEV·BBU·로봇 등 다양한 용도에 적합 
   오창에서 전동공구용 제품은 이미 양산 중  
   BBU 향 LFP 2170 은 2Q27 양산 목표로 준비 중 
   HEV 향은 2027 년 말 애리조나에서 양산 계획  
   LFP 2170 은 NCM 대비 열안전성 테스트에서 우수한 성능을 보여 고객의 긍정적 피드백 확보 
   고객 니즈·수요에 적극 대응해 신시장 경쟁력 확보 추진. 수주 규모 등 정량 정보는 제시하지 않음

Q4-1. 재무 건전성 개선 방안과 2027 년 자금 조달 계획은? 
  A. 선제 CAPA 투자 자금은 저리 정책자금 장기차입 및 원화·외화 회사채로 안정적으로 조달해 왔음 
   주요 투자는 대부분 집행됐고 CAPEX 는 2025 년을 기점으로 유의미하게 감소 중 
   향후 영업현금흐름 확대분을 차입금 상환에 우선 활용, 자산 유동화를 통한 추가 재원 확보도 병행 
   차입금·금융비용 부담을 축소해 재무지표 개선 및 건전성 확보 추진 
   2026 년 본사 추가 차입 계획은 없으며 해외법인은 기존 약정 차입금 일부 추가 인출 예정 
   2027 년은 기존 차입금 차환·운전자금 범위에서 최소한의 자금 조달 계획 
   영업현금 확대·투자 부담 완화에 따른 잉여 재원으로 차입금 감축 기조 지속. 구체적 조달 규모는 미제시

Q4-2. 유럽 IAA 정책 방향에 대응한 중장기 점유율 확보 전략은? 
  A. IAA 는 아직 최종안 미확정이나 수정 권고안상 외국인 직접투자·현지화 요건 강화 예상 
   배터리뿐 아니라 주요 소재의 현지 생산 역량 중요성이 확대될 전망 
   유럽 OEM 의 공급망 다변화로 현지 생산 체계를 갖춘 비중국 업체 소싱 니즈 확대, 경쟁 환경은 우호적 판단 
   폴란드 현지 양산 및 소재·부품 공급망을 이미 구축해 현지 배터리 소싱 수요에 신속 대응 가능 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 에 대한 유럽 고객 관심 증가. 폴란드 감가상각비 감소도 가격 경쟁력 확보에 기여 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 외 LFP·CTP 등 다양한 제품·솔루션으로 신규 수주를 확보해 유럽 점유율 확대 추진

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The U.S. Capex Boom No One is Talking About—And Its Implications
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
We have long been of the belief that the U.S. is on the cusp of an inbound FDI capex boom
that can result in numerous positive spillover effects for the U.S. economy. The latest

figures from the U.S. Department of Commerce bear this out.
Exhibit 1 highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be inside the U.S.,
with FDI totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year. That is a staggering
103% jump from a year ago and places FDI inflows on track for a record year.
The latest inbound capex already adds to the massive scale of foreign investment in the
U.S. To wit, the accumulated stock of FDI in the U.S. stands at nearly $6T, based on the

latest figures from the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA). Manufacturing accounts for
roughly 43% of the total, and, at last count, some 10,000 foreign affiliates were operating
in the U.S.
These firms are largely invisible to the average U.S. investor, but the activities of U.S.
affiliates of foreign multinationals are substantial and competitive- and growth-enhancing.
To this point and based on the latest data from the BEA, foreign affiliates in the U.S.

employed nearly 8.6 million U.S. workers in 2024. That represents 6.1% of total private
sector employment, with manufacturing and retail the two most prominent sectors
of employment. Roughly 24% of all manufacturing jobs in the U.S. are from foreign
subsidiaries. In terms of pay, U.S. workers at these firms make $89,000 annually, 7% higher
than the national average, according to the Global Business Alliance.
Foreign affiliates also contributed some $1.5T to U.S. GDP; comprised some 15.7% of total

U.S. private sector investment; accounted for 12.4% of total U.S. research & development
(R&D) expenditures; paid roughly 20% of all U.S. federal taxes; and made up 22.5% of
U.S. exports and 26.2% of U.S. imports.1 These are the direct effects of U.S. subsidiaries
of foreign companies operating in the U.S. The indirect effects are even greater in terms
of more income, jobs, growth and tax revenue—all bolstering the long-term competitive
advantage of the U.S. In a nutshell, foreign firms operating in the U.S. are an important

ingredient of U.S. economic growth.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Companies that once served the U.S. market exclusively through exports
increasingly face higher costs and more uncertainties and hence want to be “inside.”
Two, with a $32T-plus economy with extraordinary consumer purchasing power, foreign

firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment than just trade (exports). There
is no more dynamic consumer in the world than the American shopper.
Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers, semiconductors, power
generation, etc., has created numerous investment opportunities for many foreign firms
that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging capex cycle.
Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly developed

technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.
As more U.S. affiliates of foreign multinationals set up shop in the U.S., think more job
creation across the country, more manufacturing output, more locally sourced purchases,
more U.S. exports, more spending on R&D, and more tax revenue for local communities.
And over the long run, think a more globally competitive U.S. economy.
1 Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign Multinational Enterprises, 2024” and “Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign

Multinational Enterprises, 2023”, BEA July 2026.
Investment Implications
The boom in foreign investment in
the U.S. should continue to support
employment, productivity, corporate
earnings, and broader U.S. economic
competitiveness, in our view. Hence
our U.S. bias.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
And speaking of global competitiveness, Exhibit 2A is very illustrative of the magnet pull of
the U.S. economy to attract FDI versus the rest of the world. Of new greenfield investment
this decade, note the overriding preference for the U.S., accounting for nearly 16% of total
global greenfield investment this decade. America’s share is larger than the next three
countries combined—the United Kingdom, India, and Germany. And note that America’s

share of new investment is more than 5X that of China’s. Most of this new investment
has gone into manufacturing sectors like electrical equipment, computers, appliances and
batteries and has originated from Asia and Europe.
The bulk of foreign investment comes from Europe, followed by Japan and Canada, but as
Exhibit 2B highlights, there is a new dynamic playing out when it comes to America’s ability
to attract capital. There is a new source in town: a handful of developing nations (namely

Taiwan, South Korea, Mexico and Brazil). Mexican manufacturers, Taiwan technology
leaders, South Korea chip leaders, Brazil industrial firms, India pharmaceutical firms—these
companies and more are realizing that in today’s trade environment, the best way to serve
the American market is not necessarily through exports but through investment—investing
locally. FDI flows from developing nations reached $63B in the 12 months through June,
more than double the level a year prior ($24.5B), according to the BEA. Fueled by insatiable

AI demand, flows from South Korea alone totaled $23.4B in the first half of 2026. The
upshot is that an increasingly important source of capital for the U.S. is coming from an
unlikely source: the developing nations.
More fire for the capex boom. The coming boom in FDI inflows is supportive of our thesis
that the U.S. stands on the cusp of a major industrial upgrade. We are in the early innings
of a structural—not cyclical—capex cycle that should lead to the rebuilding of domestic

supply chains, higher value-added manufacturing, and more AI-driven productivity growth.
Foreign firms, in our opinion, will be part of this process.
That is important to recognize because remember the following: when a foreign company
builds a factory or an R&D facility or expands its existing manufacturing capacity right
here in the U.S., good things happen. Demand for U.S. goods and services goes up (as does
demand for construction, materials, logistics and professional services). New jobs are created,

boosting incomes of U.S. workers, while supporting more local spending and consumption.
And because many foreign firms often bring advanced manufacturing technologies when
investing in the U.S., the end results include a boost in productivity and innovation in various
sectors of the economy.
All the above, of course, are the secret ingredients to stronger U.S. economic growth, rising
corporate earnings and higher equity prices. With a little help from our friends, America’s

industrial renewal is gaining strength and momentum.

4 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MARKET VIEW
Europe’s Electoral Cycle and Market Risks
Ehiwario Efeyini, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
As midterm elections approach for the U.S. next month, voters across Europe will also
enter their own election season starting this November and continuing throughout
2027. Over the next year, 52% of the total EU population, representing 46% of EU GDP
across eight countries, will go to the polls, including voters in three of its four largest

economies (Exhibit 3). Having announced snap general elections earlier this month,
Spain will vote for members of its lower and upper house on November 29. A series of
parliamentary elections are then due for 2027 in Estonia (by March 7), Finland (on April
18), Greece (by July 25), Slovakia (by September 28), Poland (by November 11) and Italy
(by December 22). And perhaps most consequentially France will stage Presidential
elections on April 18, with a potential second round of voting set for May 2.

For the EU as a whole, the significance of these coming elections will be the potential
for a shift toward more populist, domestically oriented policy priorities from leading
opposition candidates (particularly in France, Spain and Poland) which could obstruct the
process of closer EU integration. In the nearer term, adoption of the new seven-year
(2028-2034) EU budget due by the end of 2027 could be impeded. And over the longer
term, renewed efforts toward an EU-wide capital market union to deepen and attract

more inward investment into European markets could also be further delayed.
At the country level, investors have seen bond yields rise globally in 2026 on the back of
higher inflation and concerns over fiscal sustainability. The debt question has arguably
been most pressing in parts of Europe, especially given the likelihood that upcoming
elections make for domestic increases in government largesse. And of the eight
countries going to the polls over the next year, France remains the biggest source of

concern. Interest rate spreads on French 10-year sovereign bonds have overtaken those
in both Greece and Italy this year (Exhibit 4), driven by investor concerns over public
debt. The 2027 government budget unveiled earlier this month projected a deficit of
5.0% of GDP (well in excess of the 3% specified in the EU Stability and Growth Pact),
but remained insufficiently expansive to nullify the risk that the current government
could be unseated later this year if it is forced through the fragmented parliament. This

comes on top of an existing debt load of close to 120% of GDP and a halving of official
2026 growth forecasts from 1.0% at the start of the year to 0.5% in September. In
short, all four variables in France’s debt sustainability equation currently stand as major
headwinds, and the two leading candidates in the 2027 election have shown limited
appetite for fiscal consolidation over the coming years.
Exhibit 3: Europe's Upcoming Elections Represent Around Half of EU GDP and Population.

Investment Implications
We remain cautious on European
markets overall, despite seeing
pockets of strength in selected
areas such as defense, data center
infrastructure and renewable energy.
On top of growing uncertainty
from the upcoming electoral cycle

and interest rate outlook, rising
competition and slowing demand
from China continue to challenge
key segments of the Consumer
Discretionary sector. And limited
direct AI concentration also leaves
Europe under-exposed to the key
growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and
emerging Asia.

Intervention from the ECB under its 2022 bond purchase program is also unlikely at this

stage given that the spread widening in France has not appeared either disorderly or
unwarranted. Meanwhile, conditions could potentially be aggravated by expected ECB rate
hikes in response to still above-target inflation across the eurozone. Indeed recent moves
to tighten monetary policy from a number of major central banks have been read most
hawkishly in Europe. Most of the increase in European bond yields has come from higher
inflation breakevens as opposed to real yields in the U.S. and Japan. And despite some

paring back of tightening expectations in the wake of recent bond volatility, the market is
still pricing three more policy rate increases over the next 12 months.
Euro depreciation and an emerging shift toward more protectionist trade policy with China
could further complicate the ECB policy outlook. China posted a record $35B trade surplus,
according to Eurostat, with the EU in September (alongside its $938B total surplus for the
first three quarters of 2026), and the two markets have agreed to reduce the EU bilateral

deficit by capping imports of hybrid cars for four years. Hybrids account for less than 2%
of total EU imports from China, but this could potentially mark the start of a broader shift
toward more trade protection in Europe with its largest foreign supplier which risks adding
further upward pressure to cost-push inflation. And though the leading candidates in the
French election may favor resuming oil and gas imports from Russia, Europe also remains

among the most at risk from the global energy shock. Indeed some 56% of the region’s
crude oil consumption is in the form of diesel product according to the International Energy
Agency, more than twice the U.S. share.
The 2- to 10-year eurozone yield curve has already flattened significantly since the start
of the year. And at just over 30 basis points as of mid-October, it remains flatter than
its equivalents in the U.S., UK and Japan. Outside Energy and Technology, Financials have

been among the strongest-performing sectors in Europe this year. But additional ECB
rate hikes could lead to further curve flattening, compressing bank net interest margins
and contributing to slower growth—particularly given that rate increases are coming in
response to supply-driven inflation rather than reflecting stronger economic activity.
We nonetheless continue to see pockets of strength in European markets. Growing
defense budgets should remain a tailwind for Industrials as national governments look to

meet their North Atlantic Treaty Organization targets over the next several years. And the
sector should be further supported by indirect exposure to AI investment through electric
cabling, power infrastructure and building materials for data centers. Leaders in renewable
energy equipment and services are also likely to be beneficiaries of grid upgrades and
broadening electrification globally, particularly given elevated fossil fuel prices. But our view

on the region remains cautious overall. On top of growing uncertainty from the upcoming
electoral cycle and interest rate outlook, rising competition and slowing demand from
China continue to challenge key segments of Europe’s Consumer Discretionary sector.
Limited direct AI concentration in technology hardware, cloud services and application
software also leaves Europe underexposed to the key growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and emerging Asia.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Cautious Optimism Entering Fiscal Year 2027
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
“A renewed focus on rebuilding fiscal space is urgent, given elevated debt, higher
borrowing costs, multiplying demands on public budgets and heightened uncertainty.” So
warned the IMF about the global fiscal outlook last week in its updated World Economic

Outlook report. Much is at stake for developed nations in Europe and Asia facing high
existing debt, unfavorable demographics and more modest growth.
For the U.S., between structurally higher mandatory outlays, the administration’s $1.5T
proposed defense package, and the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield sitting above 5%, spending
is likely headed higher. We continue to fade concerns of an impending U.S. debt crisis,
however, largely because economic growth continues to surprise to the upside. Expanding

output is good news for the debt-to-GDP ratio, which remains a touch below 100%.
Indeed, rather than being led higher on account of un-anchored inflation expectations, the
10-year U.S. Treasury yield has repriced to reflect better-than-expected nominal growth
(Exhibit 5A). Higher yields in the face of slowing growth, meanwhile, would be a different
story, not only for the fiscal outlook (worse) but also for U.S. equity valuations (lower).
Of course, after the U.S. accumulated another $2T deficit in FY 2026,2 “rebuilding fiscal

space” in the IMF’s words will be politically unpopular and mathematically challenging.
In our view, the biggest near-term tail risk would be a surprise move lower in growth as
budget deficits at 6% of GDP in good times leave little room for bad times. This isn’t our
base case given robust consumer spending per our internal credit and debit card data and
ongoing investment in the AI buildout and beyond. We expect these two pillars to sustain
growth in 2027.

The other good news is that, contrary to concerns, demand for Treasurys remains robust.
Yields are attractive—that was the message from October’s successful $39B 10-year
Treasury note auction. At the same time, U.S. debt boasts an array of buyers: domestic
households, the Federal Reserve, mutual funds and exchange-traded funds, money market
funds, pension funds, foreign central banks, private investors overseas, and more. Per
Exhibit 5B, whereas foreign official investors once dominated some 40% of the U.S.

Treasury market during the 2008/2009 Great Financial Crisis, their share has fallen to
12% while domestic private investors now account for a majority of holdings. Translated:
demand is more diverse than most appreciate.
Exhibit 5: Higher Yields Meet Shifting Composition of U.S. Treasury Demand.
A) Higher 10-year Yield Consistent with Strong Nominal Growth Environment. B) Domestic Private Investors Now Dominate U.S. Treasury Market.

Investment Implications
In our view, higher nominal and real
yields present a compelling entry point
for investors seeking income and can
provide cushion in the face of higher
rate volatility. We expect demand for
U.S. assets to remain healthy and favor
the U.S. over developed economies in
Europe facing more modest growth and
fiscal instability.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.
This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

美元/桶 %
► 美國經濟仍具韌性，惟通膨壓力猶存。觀察
美國9月ISM服務業指數表現，內需活動維持
穩健，企業投入成本壓力亦有所升溫；另一
方面，受地緣政治因素影響，美國柴油價格
大幅攀升，而柴油為卡車運輸、農業及製造
業的重要燃料，若價格持續維持高檔，恐進
一步增添通膨壓力。考量聯準會偏鷹立場，
且當前通膨降溫進程不如預期，年底前仍保
有升息空間。
► 美國債務總額突破40兆美元，預估2026年公
眾持有聯邦債務占GDP的比率將達101%，
財政可持續性風險升溫。與此同時，AI基礎

建設舉債熱潮，疊加中東局勢動盪帶來的通
膨不確定性，迫使投資人重新定價長天期債
券風險，要求更高的風險溢酬。隨實質殖利
率上行，美債10年期殖利率揚升並持續高懸
於5%水準之上，30年期長債殖利率亦同步站
上近20年來高點。隨長天期債券利率續揚，
殖利率曲線趨陡峭，債券配置宜先避開長天
期部位，聚焦中短天期債券鎖利機會。

### Page 6

6
投資焦點
► 距離美國期中選舉投票僅剩不到1個月，觀察
Polymarket賭盤預估數據顯示，眾議院選情
較為明朗，民主黨有超過9成的機率控制眾議
院。但參議院選情則尚有變數，今年以來兩
黨均互有領先，雖然數據顯示民主黨掌控參
議院機率呈現上揚，然隨時序進入選前最後
衝刺期，參議院能否亦由民主黨掌控仍待觀
察。故就選前而言，由於不同政黨的政策傾
向差異，潛在受惠產業亦有不同，選舉結果
的不確定性恐令投資人不易大舉押注股市。
► 另一方面，油價高檔徘迴，導致年底前通膨
擔憂仍存，加上AI企業發債需求成長、美國
財政體質惡化等因素共同發酵，推動近期美
債殖利率逐步攀高，並令股市預期本益比壓
力延續，進而限縮了投資人短期對美股的追
價動能，短期指數恐維持震盪輪動格局。可
留意抗波動、體質佳且具價值優勢之標的，
待期中選舉結束與3Q26財報展開後，不確定
性減退將有助資金回歸長線AI題材。
殖利率攀升令美股本益比面臨壓力參議院期中選舉選情仍具變數

投資焦點日股
► 9月日本銀行決議升息1碼，明確表示潛在
CPI已接近2%，未來也將繼續調高利率，政
策重點開始轉向防範通膨向上偏離目標。然
因有2位委員反對升息，加上總裁植田和男會
後談話偏向中性，令市場解讀此次升息未如
預期鷹派，美日利差不易快速縮窄，故9月會
議後日元反而呈現利多出盡的回貶反應。
► 觀察今年以來日股面臨短線日圓升值的狀態
並未出現顯著走弱態勢。一方面反映企業治
理改革推動股東報酬提升已成為支撐股價的
重要結構性因素。另一方面，升息代表日本
經濟已具備一定的健康程度，貨幣政策正常
化將不致對需求帶來衝擊。因此，隨BoJ延
續升息方向，將擴大銀行淨利差，改善其獲
利結構，並提升金融資產收益。儘管近期整
體日股預期獲利成長走勢略顯停滯，然日本
銀行股在總 體經 濟 與貨幣 政策環 境的 支持
下，獲利成長有望逆勢攀升，料將成為後續
資金關注重點。
日圓升值對日股影響似乎有限，關注銀行股獲利走強BoJ升息方向延續，惟其步伐仍取決於經濟與物價
BoJ政策保持正常化路線，日本銀行股有望持續受惠

### Page 8

8
股市
 公債殖利率走高雖可能壓抑股債市場近期動能，然而觀察美股第三季獲利年增率有望仍為雙位數成
長，或支撐股市表現。市場持續關注AI資本支出是否帶來獲利成長，短期內評價面壓力將增添市場
動盪，可分散配置於不同國家與產業，降低市場波動影響。總結上述，地緣衝突、殖利率偏高及評
價面壓力等紛擾帶來干擾，可布局防禦股增強抗震力。長期而言，科技及AI半導體發展趨勢不變且
基本面佳，可逢拉回時分批布局。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點，加上全球利率走揚有利銀行股。此外，地緣衝突頻傳，歐洲國防軍
工仍有投資題材。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融及醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，且最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升息空間，立場
偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展。殖利率偏高環境建議以中短天期債券投資為主，可逢利率走
升時布局進行鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸

引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期的鷹派，日圓震盪。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期紛擾難解，偏高的油價可能對通膨及實體經濟帶來影響。
 短期受美國雙率走高有壓，惟美日財政赤字壓力與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 短期抗波動，逢回可分批布局仍具長線趨勢之AI與科技產業

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書(或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用(境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金(不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施)；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 385: MiniMax 市值较峰值跌去近 90%

Source PDF: all report pdf/MiniMax 市值较峰值跌去近 90%.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

MiniMax 下跌并不是从某⼀次业绩爆雷开始的，也没有对应某个单⼀事件。更准确地说，
它经历的是⼀次估值体系的切换 —— 从 “ 稀缺的上市 AI 标的 ” ，回到 “ 需要证明盈利能⼒的公
司 ” 。这中间的距离，⽤了不到⼋个⽉。
摘要：
11 ⽉ 16 ⽇， MiniMax 的股价再次刷新上市以来的低点。盘中⼀度下跌 15% ，市值降⾄ 440 亿港
元。与 3 ⽉份超过 4100 亿港元的峰值相⽐，这家公司已经跌去了近 90% 。
对⼀家上市不到⼀年的公司来说，这样的曲线⾜够刺眼。但  MiniMax 的特别之处在于，它的下
跌并不是从某⼀次业绩爆雷开始的，也没有对应某个单⼀事件。更准确地说，它经历的是⼀次
估值体系的切换 —— 从 “ 稀缺的上市 AI 标的 ” ，回到 “ 需要证明盈利能⼒的公司 ” 。这中间的距离，
⽤了不到⼋个⽉。
7 ⽉ 9 ⽇，分⽔岭
股价下跌的根源可追溯到 7 ⽉ 9 ⽇。当天约 1.53 亿股解除禁售期，占这家中国 AI 开发商股票总数
的 48.9% 。接近⼀半的股份获得流通权，⽆论持有⼈是否真的想卖，市场都会提前计⼊抛压预

期。尽管多数 IPO 前投资者和基⽯投资者承诺继续持有，抛压仍迅速兑现：股价当⽇下跌
17.98% ，并在随后三个交易⽇累计下跌近 40% 。
解禁之所以成为分⽔岭，是因为它改变的是股票的供给结构。上市初期流通盘⼩，少量的买盘
就能推动价格；当近半股份解锁，定价权就从稀缺性转向了真实的供需。此后股价⼀路下⾏，
直⾄ 11 ⽉ 16 ⽇创出新低。承诺持有与现实卖压之间的落差，也说明在接近半数的股份解禁⾯
前，意愿声明很难抵消市场对流动性的重新定价。
类似的剧情在港股新股⾥并不陌⽣。上市时被反复强调的基⽯投资者承诺，往往只覆盖总股本
的⼀⼩部分，且期限有限；真正决定中期股价的，是限售期满后可流通股份的绝对规模。
MiniMax 这次解禁⽐例接近⼀半，已经超出了多数公司的常态区间。市场⽤三个交易⽇近 40%
的跌幅给出了直接回应 —— 在供给结构改变⾯前，基本⾯短期⼏乎没有发⾔权。
四年上市，⼋个⽉回撤
MiniMax 于 2026 年 1 ⽉以每股 165 港元上市，距其 2022 年成⽴仅四年左右，是 AI 领域上市最快的

公司之⼀。上市⾸⽇股价飙升 109% ，市场热情⾄此达到顶点； 3 ⽉⼀度触及 1330 港元⾼点，较
发⾏价上涨逾七倍，此后开始持续回落。
从 4100 亿港元到 440 亿港元 —— 从 3 ⽉⾼点算起，不到⼋个⽉时间，市值蒸发近九成。值得注意
的是，当前股价已经低于 165 港元的发⾏价。也就是说，打新中签并持有⾄今的投资者，不仅回
吐了全部涨幅，还处于浮亏状态；⾸⽇追⾼的买⼊者，账⾯损失更为可观。
这组数字的意义不在于⽐较谁亏得更多，⽽在于它标记了⼀轮情绪的起点和终点。上市⾸⽇的
109% 和 3 ⽉的七倍，定价的是 “AI 稀缺性 ” ；现在的 131 港元，定价的是 “ 不确定性 ” 。同⼀家公
司，在⼋个⽉⾥被两套完全不同的逻辑估了两遍。
成⽴四年即上市，本⾝就是这轮 AI 热的产物。公司需要尽早获得资本市场的弹药来⽀撑算⼒投
⼊，投资者则希望押中下⼀个平台级机会，双⽅在 2026 年初达成了⾼度⼀致的乐观预期。这种
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

预期在上市初期被放⼤，随后⼜被解禁、财报和整个板块的情绪回落逐⼀修正。回撤速度之
快，恰恰说明最初的定价⾥，有多少来⾃对未来的想象，⽽不是对当下的计算。
营收 283% ，亏损 111%
如果说股价反映的是预期，财报反映的则是现实。 2026 年上半年， MiniMax 营收同⽐增⻓
283.1% ⾄ 1.166 亿美元，已超过 2025 年全年的 7900 万美元。单看这个数字，增⻓近乎惊⼈。
但另⼀侧是：经调整后净亏损同⽐扩⼤ 111.2% ⾄ 2.93 亿美元 ——“ 营收增⻓、利润难求 ” 的问题依
然存在。营收增速 283.1% ，亏损增速 111.2% ，两个数字放在⼀起，说明亏损的绝对额仍在扩
⼤，只是增速低于营收。换句话说，规模效应还没有真正出现。
这是 AI 公司当前最典型的财务形态：收⼊曲线陡峭向上，成本曲线⼏乎同样陡峭。算⼒、模型训
练、⼈才，每⼀项都是刚性⽀出。在收⼊还没跑赢成本之前，亏损扩⼤⼏乎是必然结果。市场
此前愿意为这种形态买单，是因为相信增⻓最终会跨过临界点；当估值回到地⾯，耐⼼就会变

成需要重新论证的东西。
值得注意的是，营收 1.166 亿美元、亏损 2.93 亿美元 —— 亏损额是营收的两倍多。这个⽐例意味
着公司每获得⼀美元收⼊，就要承担超过两美元的经调整净亏损。它并⾮说明⽣意本⾝不成
⽴，开放平台和原⽣产品都有清晰的付费⽅，⽽是说明现阶段的投⼊强度远超收⼊爬坡的速
度。对⼀级市场的投资⼈，这可以解释为 “ 抢占窗⼝ ” ；对⼆级市场按季度看报表的投资者，这就
是需要逐季追问的现实压⼒。
出海，与收⼊结构
营收的构成提供了另⼀个观察⻆度。 MiniMax 的收⼊分为两⼤板块：开放平台及企业级 AI 服务
（ API 调⽤和 token 订阅）收⼊ 7393 万美元，占 63.4% ； AI 原⽣产品（如海螺 AI 和 MiniMax
Code ）收⼊ 4264 万美元。
前者是基础设施式的⽣意，靠调⽤量取胜；后者是⾯向⽤⼾的产品，靠体验和可替代性取胜。
⽬前看， API 和订阅贡献了接近三分之⼆的收⼊，说明  MiniMax 的主要客⼾仍是企业与开发者，
⽽⾮普通消费者。这⼀定位的好处是收⼊更稳定，坏处是增⻓天花板取决于客⼾的技术选型，

⽽⾮⼤众市场的⾃发扩散。
另据披露，截⾄ 6 ⽉ 30 ⽇，产品覆盖 230 多个国家和地区， 60.8% 的营收来⾃中国⼤陆以外地区
—— 国际化程度在⼤模型公司中相对较⾼，但⾼增⻓尚未转化为盈利。海外收⼊占⽐过半，某
种程度上也意味着更⾼的合规和本地化成本，这部分压⼒在财报⾥通常以销售与研发费⽤的形
式体现。
不只是  MiniMax
MiniMax 并⾮个例。包括智谱在内的其他港股⼤模型公司股价也同步下跌，投资者正在重新评
估整个⾏业的价值。
经历参数竞赛和融资热潮之后，市场的评判标准正在改变：⽐起模型规模和融资额，现在更看
重可靠的盈利路径。参数曾经是最容易讲述的故事 —— 数字越⼤，听起来越领先。但当模型能
⼒逐渐趋同，参数规模与商业回报之间的对应关系就不再成⽴，融资额也随之从加分项变成了
需要解释的成本。
更深⼀层的变化是，⼤模型越来越被视为需要⻓期投⼊的基础设施，⽽不是快速套现的通道。
基础设施的特点是前期投⼊巨⼤、回报周期漫⻓、现⾦流可预测但爆发⼒有限。⽤成⻓股的估

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

值逻辑去给基础设施定价，本⾝就会在增⻓叙事放缓时出现剧烈修正。对仍在⼤额烧钱的 AI 公司
⽽⾔，这意味着估值逻辑的重新定价。
这种重估并不等于否定 AI 的商业价值。开放平台的调⽤量在涨，原⽣产品有了⽤⼾，海外收⼊也
占到六成 —— 这些都是真实发⽣的进展。变化的只是定价的参照系：过去市场问 “ 这家公司能⻓
多⼤ ” ，现在多了⼀句 “ 它什么时候能⾃⼰养活⾃⼰ ” 。两个问题都回答得上，估值才有⽀撑；只
答得出前⼀个，股价就只能靠情绪托着，⽽情绪从来不稳定。对整个板块来说，这轮下跌更像
是⼀次被迫的成⼈礼。
结语
回到近 90% 这个数字。它⾜够醒⽬，却容易掩盖真正的变化：跌掉的不是某家公司的价值，⽽
是⼀套估值⽅法的适⽤性。把股价的每⼀次回落都归因于解禁，或把每⼀次反弹都解读为反

转，都会错过这条曲线真正传达的信息 —— 市场正在学习⽤另⼀种⽅式给 AI 定价，⽽学习的代
价，往往先由最早上市的那批公司来付。
对  MiniMax 来说， 283.1% 的营收增⻓是真实的， 2.93 亿美元的亏损也是真实的。资本市场正在
做的，是把这两件事放回同⼀个天平上重新称重。这个过程不会很快结束，也不会只发⽣在⼀
家公司⾝上。

## Document 430: Three Questions About China’s Hidden Foreign Exchange Assets (2026-11-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Three Questions About China’s Hidden Foreign Exchange Assets (2026-11-12).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 17
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
Three Questions About China’s Hidden Foreign
Exchange Assets
A trillion-dollar gap in foreign exchange purchases raises questions about where
China’s foreign currency assets are held.
Carlos Rawlins/Reuters
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
PUBLISHED
November 11, 2026 8:32 p.m.
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
The PBOC argues—unpersuasively, I think—that it
doesn’t have any hidden foreign exchange reserves. I
would reframe that as hidden foreign currency assets,
as foreign currency lent out (or swapped out) with
domestic financial institutions is a classic way of
masking reserves.
Many in Asia—and some in the IMF—are inclined
to believe the PBOC. Fair enough, it is hard to

challenge an institution as large and powerful (in
some ways) as the PBOC.

### Page 3

3
But to convince, the PBOC really needs to provide
answers to three questions.
1. What explains the current gap between foreign
exchange settlement and the PBOC’s reserves?
Settlement is the purchase of foreign exchange in the
market by the PBOC and the state banks. It
technically implies taking an open foreign currency
position, as the foreign exchange is being bought with
renminbi.
Historically, it correlated with changes in the PBOC’s
balance sheet.

And some gaps before 2012 can be explained. In late
2005 and 2006, the PBOC did over $100 billion in
swaps with the state banks, who used the FX to buy
foreign bonds (portfolio debt outflows surged). Those

### Page 4

4
swaps were unwound after the global financial crisis,
and actually provided some of the foreign currency
that “funded” the China Investment Corporation
when it initially scaled up.
And in 2007 and 2008, the PBOC required that the
state banks hold FX against their domestic RMB
deposits (to keep the FX out of reserves; it was held
on deposit at the PBOC and shows up as “other
foreign assets” on the PBOC’s balance sheet and as
“other, other, short-term” in the balance of payments.*

After 2018, though, there are unexplained gaps
between the FX bought in settlement and the
PBOC’s balance sheet (technically cumulative
monthly changes to capture the fact that FX is held as
its initial CNY purchase price).
There was a net accumulation of FX outside the
PBOC’s balance sheet in 2020 and 2021, when the
CNY was under pressure to appreciate. There are
modest sales in 2023 and 2024 when there is
depreciation pressure. And there are large purchases

in 2025 and the first 9 months of 2026—total
purchases in settlement in the last 12 months (spot)
are $500 to 600 billion, while the increase in the
PBOC’s balance sheet is only around $50 billion over
the same period.

### Page 5

5
The gap over time is now $1 trillion. The PBOC
needs to be able to answer a simple question: who
holds this FX, and who holds the associated FX
market risk (given the FX has been purchased with
RMB).
The monthly pattern of growth in settlement strongly
suggests it is backdoor intervention; there are sales
when spot is weak (economically) relative to the fix,
and purchases when spot is either at the fix or just
stronger than the fix (the strong edge of the band

hasn’t been used). The pattern of purchases and sales
thus looks exactly like that of a central bank
maintaining a tight band around a (crawling) peg.
If the PBOC cannot credibly explain this gap, there
isn’t much more to discuss. The PBOC argues that it
hasn’t swapped FX with the state banks or put foreign
currency on deposit at the state banks. Fair enough.

### Page 6

6
But someone is buying in settlement; the PBOC
ought to know who…
2. Missing interest income
There is an obvious tell here—Bloomberg reported
back in 2022 that the PBOC transferred $158 billion
in accumulated interest income to the Ministry of
Finance as part of a special dividend to hold the
budget deficit down. Again, fair enough. But
absolutely nothing changed in the FX reserves held
on the PBOC’s balance sheet or in the balance of
payments.
That highlights a broader issue. Since the end of

2016, the PBOC’s balance sheet FX hasn’t really
changed.

### Page 7

7
Peer institutions generally have an upward slope to
their reserves as a result of “gains from reserve
management” or “reinvestment of interest income.” **
The Central Bank of China (Taipei) and Japan’s
Ministry of Finance are good examples.
The interest on ~$3 trillion in reserves should be
around $100 billion a year. A bit less during the “low-
for-long” era before the pandemic, when SAFE wasn’t
getting much income out of its euro portfolio. A bit

more now, when U.S. short-term rates are well above 3
percent and SAFE’s legacy agencies carry yields of 5
or 6 percent. $100 billion over 10 years is real money
—roughly a trillion dollars.
If SAFE has steadily been selling its interest income,
it should say so (the fact that BOP reserves have often
diverged from the PBOC balance sheet suggests a
more complex story).

### Page 8

8
Moreover, if SAFE has been transferring the FX to
one of China’s many sovereign wealth funds
(including those controlled by SAFE) or the policy
banks, it should say so.
But SAFE cannot say what you see is what we really
have if it cannot explain how it handles the interest
on its now $3.5 trillion in reserves.
3. The accounting for the PBOC’s entrusted loans
and other foreign currency claims on domestic
financial institutions, notably the policy banks

FX handed over to a sovereign wealth fund to manage
doesn’t appear in reserves. It isn’t a liquid reserve asset.
But it should appear elsewhere in the balance of
payments as either portfolio debt or portfolio equity.
And FX handed over to the policy banks through
mechanisms such as entrusted loans (or a simple
deposit or swap) doesn’t count as a foreign reserve—
the foreign currency claim is on a domestic financial
institution, and that isn’t considered a “foreign” asset.

There is no doubt that the PBOC has domestic
claims on China’s financial institutions. The use of
“entrusted” loans to fund the policy banks in 2010
was reported by Caixin and the Financial Times.

### Page 9

9
$95 billion in entrusted loans were converted into
equity in the CDB and China Exim back in 2015
(giving SAFE majority equity stakes in both
institutions through its Buttonwood investment
vehicle).
And the SAFE’s 2020 annual report contained a ten-
year progress report on the “diversified use” of China’s
FX (basically, the non-reserve use) which indicated
that SAFE continued to provide entrusted loans to
small, medium, and large Chinese financial

institutions.
Now perhaps these instruments have been unwound
—the China Development Corporation (per Stephen
Green, a friend) has reduced its foreign currency
balance sheet over the last few years. But that should
lead the foreign currency to flow back to SAFE
absent another use—and that increase in FX isn’t
showing up in the PBOC’s reserves.
In other words, SAFE should provide a transparent
accounting of the FX that has been bought but
transferred to other financial institutions and any

ongoing foreign currency exposure to the domestic
banking system (including the policy banks).
These aren’t big asks. The settlement gap is big and
obvious, interest income is a known issue, and SAFE
itself has reported its use of entrusted loans in the
past. There is no issue around the past use of swaps

### Page 10

10
either. A substantial share of the banks’ foreign
currency assets are funded domestically. It should be
possible for SAFE to explain the role, if any, it has
played in this funding.
There is, of course, another issue around China’s
balance of payments: the growth in foreign currency
external assets funded out of domestic foreign
currency deposits.
China argues that the growing foreign assets of its
state banks are simply a natural part of a growing

domestic financial system. But there is more to the
story.
Onshore FX deposits have tended to rise when
economics say they should not, and fall when the
economics say they should (offshore FX deposits
behave as expected). In 2020 and 2021, FX deposits

### Page 11

11
rose even when there was more yield on RMB
deposits.
And RMB deposits have continued to rise in 2025
and 2026 even with the RMB’s appreciation
offsetting the higher dollar deposit rate. This isn’t an
accident: banks just raised the interest rate that they
pay on dollar deposits to encourage exporters NOT to
convert their export proceeds into RMB and thus
take pressure off the PBOC (per Reuters).
In other words, the pattern of the banks’ foreign

currency asset growth suggests that they are offsetting
appreciation pressure by bidding for FX (or receiving
it from other state actors), not that FX flows into the
banks during periods when onshore residents worry
about depreciation.

### Page 12

12
Right now, the “settlement gap” is the most pressing
issue. FX deposit growth in excess of FX loan growth
can explain FX outflows of around $150 billion in the
four quarters to Q3. The FX outflows implied by
settlement reached $600 billion over the last four
quarters. Together they account for the bulk of
China’s reported $825 billion plus (I estimate a Q4
2025 to Q3 2026 surplus of around $900 billion) in
outflows implied by the reported current account.***

### Page 13

13
It goes without saying that this matters because the
weak RMB is the central reason for China’s massive
export outperformance since 2023—and that export
outperformance has continued in 2026.****
* I have been doing this a long time; I actually do
know most of the historical quirks in China’s balance

### Page 14

14
of payments data.
** The MoF can count a portion of its unrepatriated
gains from accumulated interest on its large reserves
against the budget deficit, but since the actual
interest is held abroad, it still needs to borrow
domestically against those reserves (domestic JGB
issuance and foreign reserves both go up).
*** My estimate of a larger “true” current account
comes from moving gold imports over to the financial
account, and from an assumption that undercounting

of the reinvested earnings of funds held offshore
explains the obvious problem in the investment
income account.
**** The spike in imports in Q1 wasn’t entirely real.
China was slow to adjust its import price index for
the rise in semiconductor/chip prices. This inflated
both import and export volumes in Q1. The rise in
gold imports this year also registers in the import
volume series—though the rise is best understood as
“importing” a financial asset.

### Page 15

15
This work represents the views solely of the author(s). The
Council on Foreign Relations is an independent,
nonpartisan membership organization, think tank, and
publisher, and takes no institutional positions on matters
of policy.

《中東衝突升溫，地緣風險升國防優於航空運輸》
受到中東葉門胡塞武裝攻擊沙烏地多座機場，及烏
克蘭無人機攻擊俄羅斯能源設施導致布蘭特原油價
格10月再度攀升、帶動航空燃油價格同步上漲，高
油價再次衝擊航空運輸公司獲利。達美航空3Q26財
報，預告4Q26單位可用座位里程收入仍將增加，但
受到美國4Q26航空客運需求將下滑，以及航空燃油
價格上漲。達美航空也下調2026年全年獲利目標，
顯示地緣風險升溫再度衝擊航空運輸產業。
至於航空與國防產業受益於中東衝突與俄烏戰爭持
續消耗防空攔截器、戰術飛彈及相關彈藥，帶動美

國及盟國由短期補庫存逐步轉向多年期採購。全球
國防支出增加、飛彈與防空系統補庫存，以及商用
航空引擎與維修需求持續成長，也讓產業巨頭雷神
(簡稱RTX)、奇異航天(簡稱GE)、洛克希德馬丁(簡
稱 LMT) 、 諾 斯 洛 普 ( 簡稱NOC) 等 公 司 積 壓 訂 單
(Backlog)於3Q26均創歷史新高。

重點摘要
• 國防需求已由短期補庫存轉
為多年期擴產
《國防需求已由短期補庫存轉為多年期擴產》
美國主要航空與國防類股公布3Q26財報，中東衝突
再升溫導致國防需求高於商業航空。以下是主要廠
商的分析 :
雷神∶受益於飛彈回補庫存，3Q26取得超過810億
美元商用與國防新訂單，其中國防訂單590億美元，
包括約230億美元「戰斧」飛彈訂單與140億美元
「先進中程空對空飛彈」（AMRAAM）訂單，帶
動總積壓訂單較上季增加520億美元，累計訂單
3,400億美元，年增36%。在飛彈防空系統與商業
航空維修服務市場強勁帶動下，公司上調2026年營
收到973億美元、EPS 7.70美元(年增22%)。
洛克希德馬丁∶受益於飛彈與火控系統產量提升，

帶動3Q26營收與獲利優於預期。在PAC-3多年期合
約則推升下，積壓訂單增加616億美元達2,920億美
元。由於美國、中東、北約國家飛彈補庫需求將持
續數年，所以公司上調2026年營收到810億美元(年
增8%)、EPS 30.55美元(年增42%)。
奇異航空∶受益於LEAP交付、備件、維修及shop
visit增加帶動，商用發動機及服務營收年增21%，
3Q26營收優於預期。在商用航空備件、維修服務及
引擎交付需求優於預期，加上主要供應商材料投入
改善， 公司上調2026 年營收到508億美元、EPS
7.75美元(年增26%)。
總結，雖然國防類股近期財報受益於產能提升維幅
上調全年財測，反觀油價高漲對於美國航空運輸業
造成全面性的衝擊，所以工業維持中立評等。
資料來源：公司，2026/10/28，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28

重點摘要
3
• 五 中 全 會 公 報 隱 含 官 方
對 完 成 年 度 經 濟 目 標 具
相 當 信 心 ， 短 期 推 出 大
規 模 景 氣 刺 激 政 策 可 能
性降低。
• 五 中 全 會 前 後 顯 示 本 輪
政 治 整 頓 力 度 短 期 有 所
降溫， 但 整 體 水 準 仍 明
顯高於歷史均值。
《投資關鍵-反腐短暫趨緩，關注4Q景氣能否達標》
中國第三季實質GDP年增4.5%，前三季累計年增
4.6%，位居官方全年4.5%至5.0%目標區間下緣，
整體延續外強內弱格局。出口強勁，加上政策刺激
提供經濟底部支撐，然投資與消費兩大內需引擎同
步降溫，內生需求不足的結構性問題未有效改善。
展望後市，官方將擴大超長期特別國債與地方政府

專項債發行規模，加快重大基建項目落地，並持續
透過房地產去庫存、城中村改造等定向措施穩定內
需，搭配結構性寬鬆政策引導融資成本下行。惟五
中全會公報強調「確保完成全年經濟社會發展主要
目標任務」來看，官方對完成年度成長目標具相當
信心，因此第四季GDP年增率僅需維持約4.5%至
4.6%，短期推出大規模景氣刺激政策可能性降低。
本次五中全會主要聚焦黨的治理體系、監督機制與
自我革命等議題，並未釋出新一輪大規模財政或貨
幣刺激訊號。此外，觀察高盛編製的反腐敗代理指
標，五中全會前後雖略有回落，顯示本輪政治整頓
力度短期有所降溫，但整體水準仍明顯高於歷史均
值。隨著2027年新一屆黨領導層改組逐步接近，反
腐與監管力道仍有再度升溫的可能。在此背景下，
地方官員風險偏好與投資意願恐持續受到抑制，進
而削弱政策寬鬆措施向實體經濟的傳導效果，使中
國經濟仍面臨政策支持與內生需求不足並存的挑戰。
留意反貪腐力道若再度上升將壓抑政策傳導效果
資料來源：高盛，2026/10/30。中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/11/2。

《投資關鍵-回測顯示美期中選後短線有反彈契機》
美國11/3將舉行參眾兩院的國會選舉，除視為對
當任總統的支持度檢驗之一，參眾兩院的黨派席次
也可能影響接下來政策推行速度。
回顧過去美股期中選舉期間，新興市場選前大多表
現壓抑，惟選後大多繳出正報酬佳績，上漲比率超
越五成。若以參眾兩院控制權皆翻轉的兩個年度
1994年、2006年來看，1944年因美聯準會啟動快
速升息循環，加上墨西哥金融危機爆發，導致選後
新興市場進入近一年的空頭走勢。然2006年即便
也是分裂政府，全球宏觀環境正受惠全球貿易繁榮、
商品超級周期推動經濟成長，新興市場選後12個

月累積報酬相當亮眼。
資料來源：Bloomberg，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28。

重點摘要
5
• 同樣面臨美國政治版圖翻
轉，新興市場指數表現卻
顯著差異，意味選後更取
決於景氣與獲利週期，而
非國會席次變化本身。
• 過去美股期中選舉前。台
股的平均表現最為壓抑，
但選後反彈幅度亦最為顯
著，且正報酬機率維持在
較高水準。
比較1994年與2006年同樣面臨美國政治版圖翻轉，
指數表現卻顯著差異，意味選後更取決於景氣與獲
利週期，而非國會席次變化本身。
進一步檢視1990年以來美國期中選舉的各市場表
現，中國上證指數受自身政策與景氣循環影響較深，
與美國期中選舉關聯性相對有限外，多數市場皆呈
現選前承壓、選後回升的特徵。其中，台股於期中
選舉前的平均表現最為壓抑，但選後反彈幅度亦最
為顯著，且正報酬機率維持在較高水準。或反應美

國政治不確定性於選後下降，市場焦點重新回歸景
氣與企業獲利基本面，帶動資金自美國逐步向Beta
值較高的市場擴散。進一步而言，若相關國家具備
較穩健的經濟基本面、企業獲利動能或產業成長題
材支撐，則更有機會在風險偏好回升與資金重新配
置的過程中，成為資金優先流入的受惠對象
資料來源：Bloomberg，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28。

重點摘要
6
• 美期中選對新興市場影響
較大的政策面向主要包括
關稅與貿易政策、債務上
限議題，以及政府對資料
中心建設與AI相關投資的
態度。
• 即使選後對資料中心的監
管聲浪升高，政策方向更
可能聚焦於強化治理框架
與技術管制，而非限制境
內AI投資或資料中心建設。
《投資關鍵-期中選舉後關注政策推動能力的變遷》
另一個觀察美國期中選舉後政黨版圖變化對新興市
場影響的角度，在於其對政策推動能力的改變。美
期中選對新興市場影響較大的政策面向主要包括關
稅與貿易政策、債務上限議題，以及政府對資料中
心建設與AI相關投資的態度。
首先，在關稅與貿易政策方面，由於關稅、地緣政
治及貿易談判等政策多屬行政權限範疇，政策方向
仍由白宮主導，且部分政策工具並不高度依賴國會

授權，因此政策波動性仍需關注。
其次，債務上限與財政融資需求則是影響資金流向
的重要觀察指標。國會僵局或限制積極的財政政策，
但也可能讓債務上限僵局上演，擾動市場風險偏好。
最後，近期美國各地對資料中心的反對聲浪升溫，
主要集中耗電、用水及對當地生活環境的影響。然
而，資料中心能否順利興建，關鍵仍掌握在州政府、
公共事業委員會及地方政府手中，因其負責電力、
水資源、土地分區與稅務優惠等審批事項。因此，
影響AI基建進度的主要變數未必是聯邦層級的期中
選舉，而更可能來自地方政府的政策取向。另一方
面，美國已將AI視為攸關國家競爭力、經濟成長與
國家安全的關鍵基礎設施，因此即使選後監管聲浪
升高，政策方向更可能聚焦於強化治理框架與技術
管制，而非限制境內AI投資或資料中心建設。換言
之，選後最大的差異較可能體現在建設進度與行政
程序，而非AI投資趨勢本身出現明顯逆轉。對以科
技硬體與AI供應鏈為主的新興亞洲市場而言，企業
獲利與資本支出動能仍將是比國會版圖變化更重要
的驅動因素。

6月底啟動三大超級計畫後，韓國政府
加快推出相關配套措施，宣佈設立5兆
韓元半導體專項基金，重點投資芯片材

年計畫，促進大企業和較小供應商合作
如半導體研發、測試及生產等領域。再
者費外成為對經濟貢獻的推手之一。
重點摘要
7
• 期中選舉落幕後，焦點重
回美伊戰事若開打，油價
回升恐再度引發高利環境
的擔憂。
《投資關鍵-以長線投資視野聚焦穩健獲利標的》
展望後市，新興市場第四季仍將面臨波動升溫的環
境。 從歷史經驗與政策面觀察，美國期中選舉後
政治不確定性雖有望下降，但市場焦點最終仍將回
歸總體經濟、企業獲利與流動性環境。據傳美以軍

方擬定在美國期中選舉後再攻擊伊朗，而這可能再
度推升油價，進而引發市場對高利環境的擔憂。
基本面方面，市場預估新興市場經濟成長率將由
2026年第三季的3.4%升至第四季的4.4%，顯示整
體景氣仍具韌性。然而，高利率環境維持時間長於
預期，正考驗各國內需與投資動能；同時，已開發
國家央行偏鷹立場未見明顯鬆動，也使新興市場進
一步降息與推動寬鬆政策的空間受到壓縮。市場對
2027年新興市場景氣與企業獲利的預期差異恐將
擴大，資產表現也將更加分化。
台灣涵蓋晶圓代工、IC設計、先進封裝、AI伺服器、
PCB及網通設備等完整AI供應鏈。多層次且廣泛的
產業布局，不僅有助於分散單一產業景氣波動風險，
也使AI投資動能更全面傳導至企業獲利與出口表現，
資料來源：Bloomberg，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/28。
